yato janmasthitilayā bhavanti jagato'sya tat || 26 ||
brahmeti lakṣaṇaṃ janmakāraṇatvādi kathyate |
brahma lakṣaṇato vākyāt pratipattuṃ na śakyate || 27 ||
viśeṣaṇatvena vopalakṣaṇatvena vā tathā |
janmādīnāṃ brahmaṇo'sya lakṣaṇatvaṃ na saṃbhavet || 28 ||
viśeṣaṇatvapakṣe hi teṣāṃ vyāvartakatvataḥ |
vyāvartitaṃ tribhistaiśca brahmā'nekaṃ bhavettathā || 29 ||
dvitīyepyupalakṣyasya jñātākāro hyapekṣitaḥ |
brahmaṇastadabhāvācca pakṣopyeṣa na saṅgataḥ || 30 ||
itthaṃ lakṣaṇato brahma pratipattuṃ na śakyate |
ato jijñāsyatvamasya neti vādo'samañjasaḥ || 31 ||
aviruddhaiḥ kālabhedājjanmakāraṇatādibhiḥ |
viśeṣaṇairbrahmaṇo'sya bahutvāpādanaṃ na sat || 32 ||
śrīpatitvādinā'nyatra pratipannaṃ ca vastu tat |
jñāpyate brahmaśabdārthatayā tairlakṣaṇairiha || 33 ||
tasmājjanmasthemalayakāraṇatvatrayaṃ pṛthak |
lakṣaṇaṃ brahmaṇo'sya syāt saṃbhūyāpi ca tadbhavet || 34 ||
|| iti janmādyadhikaraṇam ||
|| śāstrayonitvādhikaraṇam ||
|| śāstrayonitvāt ||
yadyapi brahma śakyaṃ tatpratipattuṃ ca lakṣaṇaiḥ |
tathāpi tadbrahma vedyamanumānapramāṇataḥ || 35 ||
mānāntarāvedyameva śāstravedyaṃ bhavedataḥ |
yata ityādivākyānāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ nātra saṅgatam || 36 ||
maivaṃ siṣādhayiṣitaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvaśaktikam |
jīvādvilakṣaṇaṃ brahma na siddhyedanumānataḥ || 37 ||
ato'nyamānāvedyatvāt śāstraikaviṣayatvataḥ |
brahmaṇastatra śāstrāṇāṃ prāmāṇye kāpi na kṣatiḥ || 38 ||
|| iti śāstrayonitvādhikaraṇam ||
|| samanvayādhikaraṇam ||
|| tattu samanvayāt ||
aprāptatve'pyanyamānaiḥ pravṛttyādāvananvayāt |
brahmaṇassiddharūpatvāccāprayojanarūpataḥ || 39 ||
śāstrasya tasminprāmāṇyāsaṃbhavāttadvibodhyatā |
nāsya saṃbhavatītyevaṃ vādo'tīvā'samañjasaḥ || 40 ||
śāstrapramāṇakatvaṃ tat puruṣārthatayā'nvayāt |
brahmaṇassaṃbhavatyeva vedāntāḥ puruṣaṃ param || 41 ||
nirgatāvadhikānandasvarūpaṃ bodhayanti hi |
guṇavatputrapitrādijīvanajñānavattathā || 42 ||
brahmajñānamapīdṛkṣaṃ puruṣārthātmakaṃ bhavet |
atyutkṛṣṭapumarthe'smin paramānandade svayam || 43 ||
ānandarūpe śāstrasya bodhakatvena cānvayāt |
śāstrapramāṇakatvaṃ tat brahmaṇastasya siddhyati || 44 ||
|| iti samanvayādhikaraṇam ||
|| īkṣatyadhikaraṇam || 
|| īkṣaternāśabdam ||
itthaṃ kṛtaṃ catussūtryā śāstrāraṃbhasamarthanam |
pradhānādeḥ kāraṇatvavādo'tha kṣipyate kramāt || 45 ||
sadvidyāyāṃ kāraṇatvaṃ prakṛteḥ śrūyate'citaḥ |
kathaṃ jagatkāraṇasya puruṣārthatvasaṃbhavaḥ || 46 ||
ityuktiśamanāyā'bhūdīkṣatyādhikṛtistataḥ |
guṇatrayātmakasyāsya jagato vastu tādṛśam || 47 ||
upādānaṃ bhavetkāryasājātyaṃ syāttathā sati |
tatteja aikṣatetyādau gauṇekṣāśravaṇāttathā || 48 ||
tadaikṣateti vākyepi gauṇamevekṣaṇaṃ bhavet |
atra pratijñādṛṣṭāntakathanāccānumānikam || 49 ||
pradhānameva sacchabdavedyamityādyasanmatam |
sacchabdārthaḥ pradhānaṃ na sata īkṣitṛtāśruteḥ || 50 ||
brahmaṇaścidacinmūrtessarvasājātyasaṃbhavāt |
mukhyekṣāsaṃbhavāccātra hetorapratipādanāt || 51 ||
anumānāpratīteśca cchāndogyaśrutigocare |
sadvidyāvādivākye satpadoktaḥ paraḥ pumān || 52 ||
|| gauṇaścennātmaśabdāt ||
gauṇī tadaikṣatetīkṣā gauṇekṣāsāhacaryataḥ |
maivaṃ sacchabdavācye'smin īkṣitaryātmaśabdataḥ || 53 ||
mukhyaivekṣā'trā''tmagatā teja āderapīkṣaṇam |
mukhyaṃ sadātmakatvāttasyai tadātmyamiti śruteḥ || 54 ||
|| tanniṣṭhasya mokṣopadeśāt ||
sadupāsananiṣṭhasya jīvasya paramaṃ phalam |
mokṣākhyaṃ śrūyate'taśca sadvācyaṃ brahma na tvajā || 55 ||
|| heyatvāvacanācca ||
yadīha kāraṇatayā pradhānaṃ tadvivakṣitam |
tasya mokṣavirodhitvāducyetā'muṣya heyatā || 56 ||
na cocyate heyatā'taḥ pradhānaṃ na vivakṣitam |
|| pratijñāvirodhāt ||
yenāśrutaṃ śrutamiti pratijñā'tra viruddhyate || 57 ||
sadvijñānena cidacitprapañcajñānamatra hi |
pratijñātaṃ pradhānaṃ cedvācyaṃ jīvagaṇānprati || 58 ||
pradhānasyākāraṇatvāt jñāyante te na taddhiyā |
tasmādasaṅgatā sā syātpratijñā'taśca na tvajā || 59 ||
|| svāpyayāt ||
suṣuptasya citassvīyahetau satyapyayaśrutiḥ |
viruddhā syātpradhānasyākāraṇatvāccitaṃ prati || 60 ||
|| gatisāmānyāt ||
itaropaniṣadvākyasamānārthatayā'sya ca |
kāraṇaṃ na pradhānaṃ syātparaṃ brahmaiva kāraṇam || 61 ||
|| śrutatvācca ||
ātmataḥ prāṇa ityādi śrūyate'traiva sāmani |
ataḥ prādhānādevānyatsarvajñaṃ brahma kāraṇam || 62 ||
|| iti īkṣatyadhikaraṇam ||
|| ānandamayādhikaraṇam ||
|| ānandamayo'bhyāsāt ||
acetanasya mukhyekṣā'saṃbhavāttanna kāraṇam |
ityuktaṃ tarhi jīvo'stvityākṣepo'tra nirasyate || 63 ||
ānandavallyāṃ vijñānamayādanyo'ntarastathā |
ātmā''nandamayastene tyādivākyaṃ prapaṭhyate || 64 ||
sa cānandamayo jīvastasya śārīratāśruteḥ |
jīvāsādhāraṇaṃ tacca śārīratvamato'sya ca || 65 ||
atrekṣāpūrvikāsṛṣṭiścetanatvātsusaṅgatā |
maivamatrānandamayaḥ paro jīvādvilakṣaṇaḥ || 66 ||
nirgatātiśayatvenā'bhyastānandasya yogataḥ |
tādṛśānandayogitvaṃ jīvasyāsya na saṃbhavet || 67 ||
śārīratvaṃ mukhyameva brahmaṇaścidacittanoḥ |
tasmādatrānandamayaḥ paramātmeti gīyate || 68 ||
|| vikāraśabdānneti cenna prācuryāt ||
mayaṭo'sya vikārārthatayā'trāvikṛtaḥ paraḥ |
na vācya iti cennaitanmayaṭ prācuryabodhakaḥ || 69 ||
tādṛgeva mayaṭ pūrvaṃ prakṛto'taḥ paraḥ pumān |
yato vāca iti proktānandaprācuryavānayam || 70 ||
|| taddhetuvyapadeśācca ||
jīvānandayitṛtvaṃ cāsyai ṣahyeveti vākyataḥ |
ucyate'taśca jīvātsyādanya ānandarūpyayam || 71 ||
|| māntravarṇikameva ca gīyate ||
satyamityādimantroktaṃ brahmaiva hyatra gīyate |
ānandamayaśabdenātaścandamayaḥ paraḥ || 72 ||
|| netaro'nupapatteḥ ||
mantravarṇodito na syāñjīvo yo brahmaṇaḥ paraḥ |
brahmaikāntavipaścittvadharmasyānupapattitaḥ || 73 ||
|| bhedavyapadeśācca ||
tasmādvā iti vākyena vijñānamayaśadbitāt |
asyānandamayasyokto bhedo jīvādataḥ paraḥ || 74 ||
|| kāmācca nānumānāpekṣā ||
saṅkalpena jagatsṛṣṭirnājāpekṣā'sya vidyate |
jīvasya tadapekṣā'lpepyataścāyaṃ paraḥ pumān || 75 ||
|| asminnasya ca tadyogaṃ śāsti ||
ānandayoga ānandamaye jīvasya kathyate |
rasaṃ hyevetyādivākyāttasmājjīvānya iṣyate || 76 ||
|| ityānandamayādhikaraṇam ||
|| antaradhikaraṇam ||
|| antastaddharmopadeśāt ||
jīvasāmānyasya pūrvaṃ kāraṇatvaṃ nirākṛtam |
tadviśeṣe tadāśaṅkya nyāye'muṣminnirasyate || 77 ||
chāndogye yo ya eṣo'ntariti vākye śrutaḥ pumān |
akṣyādityādhāratayā sa ca jīvo vilakṣaṇaḥ || 78 ||
śarīravatvaśravaṇāt jīvā eva hi tadyujaḥ |
maivamakṣyādityavartī paramātmaiva netaraḥ || 79 ||
upadeśādapahatapāpmatvādiguṇāvaleḥ |
śarīravatvaṃ na syāddhi karmavaśyatvasādhakam || 80 ||
aprākṛtassvānurūpo divyamaṅgalavigrahaḥ |
svecchāgṛhīta evāstītyāmnāyairjñāyate'khilaiḥ || 81 ||
|| bhedavyapadeśāccānyaḥ ||
ya ātmanītyādivākyairādityamukhajīvataḥ |
bhedopadeśādanyo'ntarātmā nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 82 ||
|| ityantaradhikaraṇam ||
|| ākāśādhikaraṇam ||
|| ākāśastalliṅgāt ||
kāraṇaṃ vastu nirṇītaṃ cidacidbhyāṃvilakṣaṇam |
tadasaṅgatamityeṣa vādo'tra vinivāryate || 83 ||
sāmni sarvāṇi he tyādau kāraṇatvena saṃśrutaḥ |
ākāśo viyadeva syāt loke tatra prayogataḥ || 84 ||
kuto bhūtāni jāyanta ityanirdhāraṇe sati |
ākāśāditivākyaṃ syādviśeṣārthasamarpakam || 85 ||
maivamatrākāśaśabdavācyaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ |
viyadanyastadekāntaliṅgānāṃ bhūyasāṃ śruteḥ || 86 ||
siddhānuvādarūpaṃ ca śrūyate vākyamatra hi |
ataḥ pramāṇāntarasya prāptimetadapekṣate || 87 ||
bodhayatyakhilaṃ mānaṃ brahma nārāyaṇāhvayam |
vyutpatyā''kāśata iti tasya tacchadbavācyatā || 88 ||
|| ityākāśādhikaraṇam ||
|| prāṇādhikaraṇam ||
|| ata eva prāṇaḥ ||
pūrvatra viyatassarvakāraṇatvaṃ nirākṛtam |
nirasyate'tra vāyośca kāraṇatvaṃ paroditam || 89 ||
sāmni sarvāṇi he tyādi śrūyate vākyamatra ca |
ākāśaśabdavatprāṇaśabdopi brahmabodhakaḥ || 90 ||
parāsādhāraṇālliṅgātprasiddhavadudāhṛtāt |
śaṅkā'dhikā tvatra bhūtajātasthityādikasya ca || 91 ||
prāṇādhīnatvato vāyurbhūtaṃ kāraṇamastviti |
parihāraśśilādau ca citsvarūpe ca neti tat || 92 ||
|| iti prāṇādhikaraṇam ||
|| jyotiradhikaraṇam ||
|| jyotiścaraṇābhidhānāt ||
nirākṛtyā''kāśavāyvoḥ kāraṇatvamatha kramāt |
tejasaḥ kāraṇatvaṃ ca nyāye'muṣminnirasyate || 93 ||
ataḥ paro divo jyotiriti vākye'tidīptimat |
śrūyate tatkāraṇaṃ syātkaukṣeyajyotiṣā saha || 94 ||
aikyopadeśāccātroktaṃ prasiddhajyotirastu tat |
maivaṃ jyotiśśabdavācyamatra brahmaiva netarat || 95 ||
pūrvavākye sarvabhūtacaraṇatvābhidhānataḥ |
tacca brahmaṇa eva syātpuṃsūktādiprasiddhitaḥ || 96 ||
chandobhidhānānneti cenna tathā cetorpaṇanigamāttathāhi
darśanam ||
pūrvavākyoktagāyatrīchanda evātra gīyate |
ṛcā tadarthabodhinyā na tat brahmeti cenna tat || 97 ||
pare phalāya gāyatrītaulyadṛṣṭirvidhīyate |
catuṣpado brahmaṇo'sya tādṛśyā ca tayā tulā || 98 ||
dṛṣṭassaṃvargavidyādau taulye cchandovaconvayaḥ |
|| bhūtādipādavyapadeśopapatteścaivam || 
saiṣā catuṣpadetyuktirbhūtādyaṅghritvabodhikā || 99 ||
gāyatrīśabdavācye'smin brahmaṇyevopapadyate |
|| upadeśabhedānneti cennobhayasminnapyavirodhāt ||
divo divīti bhinnārthavibhaktivyatirekataḥ || 100 ||
pratyabhijñāyate nātra brahma pūrvatra saṃśrutam |
maivamarthasvabhāvaikyādupadeśadvayepi ca || 101 ||
abādhātpratyabhijñāyāḥ para evātra kathyate |
kaukṣeyaikyopadeśastu phalāyaiveti niścitam || 102 ||
paramātmātmakatvaṃ ca gītāproktaṃ vibhāvasoḥ |
tasmātsa paramātmaivetyetadatra suniścitam || 103 ||
|| iti jyotiradhikaraṇam ||
|| indraprāṇādhikaraṇam ||
|| prāṇastathānugamāt ||
jyotiśśabditasūryādeḥ kāraṇatvaṃ nirākṛtam |
tat patitvena siddhasya devendrasya nirasyate || 104 ||
pratardano heti vākye kauṣītakyabhidhaśrutau |
muktyupāsanakarmatvenendraprāṇapaderitaḥ || 105 ||
jīva evendraśabdasya tasminnava prasiddhitaḥ |
tathaiva prāṇavāco'pi svātmopāsanamatra saḥ || 106 ||
upādiśaddhitatamamamṛtatvāptisādhanam |
kāraṇopāsanasyaivāmṛtatvaprāptihetutā || 107 ||
tasya tāvaditi śrutyā jñāyate'taśca kāraṇam |
prasiddhajīvabhāvo'yamindra eveti niścayaḥ || 108 ||
maivamindraprāṇaśabdyaṃ jīvamātraṃ na kintvataḥ |
arthāntaraṃ brahma tābhyāṃ padābhyāṃ prastutasya ca || 109 ||
svabhāvasiddhānandatvāmṛtatvādeśca saṃśruteḥ |
|| na vakturātmopadeśāditi cedadhyātmasambandhabhūmā hyasmin ||
prajñātajīvabhāvasya svasyaivopāsyatāmayam || 110 ||
vadatyato jīva evopāsyaḥ prakrānta ucyate |
maivamatra śrutā dharmāḥ parasminneva saṅgatāḥ || 111 ||
mokṣārthopāstikarmatvaṃ sarvādhāratvamapyatha |
karmakārayitṛtvaṃ cetyādyā dharmāḥ parasya hi || 112 ||
|| śāstradṛṣṭyā tūpadeśo vāmadevavat ||
antaryāmibrāhmaṇādervijñāyā''tmaśarīritām |
anena jīvenetyādyaiḥ śabdānāṃ jīvavācitām || 113 ||
pare paryavasānaṃ ca māmupāsveti vākyataḥ |
svadehakaṃ paraṃ brahmopāsyatvenopadiṣṭavān || 114 ||
vāmadevādayopyevaṃ vadantyadhyātmavidvarāḥ |
ahaṃ manurahaṃ sarvamityādibhiranekadhā || 115 ||
|| jīvamukhyaprāṇaliṅgānneti cennopāsātraividhyādāśritatvādiha
tadyogāt ||
jīvamukhyaprāṇagataliṅgānāmatra darśanāt |
pūrvoktādhyātmasambandhabhūmnā nātrārthaniścayaḥ || 116 ||
maivamatropāsanasya trividhatvābhisandhitaḥ |
svasvarūpeṇa jīvatmaśarīrakatayā tathā || 117 ||
prāṇāṅgakatvenetthaṃ ca parātmopāsanaṃ tridhā |
tridhā tadupadeśāya tattacchabdena bhāṣaṇam || 118 ||
iyaṃ ca trividhopāstiranyatrāpi śrutau śrutā |
ataḥ prakaraṇaṃ caitattādṛśopāstibodhakam || 119 ||
ato'trendraprāṇaśabdavācyo jīvetaraḥ paraḥ |
bhoktṛbhogyaśarīro'tra paramātmopadiśyate || 120 ||
|| iti indraprāṇādhikaraṇam ||
|| iti prathamādhyāye prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| prathamādhyāye dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| sarvatraprasiddhyadhikaraṇam || 
sarvatra prasiddhopadeśāt 
aspaṣṭatarajīvādiliṅgavākyaṃ vicāritam |
vicāryate vākyajātaṃ pāde'trāspaṣṭaliṅgakam || 121 ||
kāraṇatvamupāsyatvaṃ coktaṃ prāṇaśarīriṇaḥ |
jīvasya syāttadubhayamityāśaṃkya nirasyate || 122 ||
sarvaṃ khalvidamityādau sarvātmakatayā śrutaḥ |
jīva eva yato'syaiva sarvatādātmyasaṃbhavaḥ || 123 ||
brahmādisarvabhāvaśca tasyaiva syādupādhitaḥ |
parasyā'spṛṣṭadoṣasya tadbhāvo nopapadyate || 124 ||
jīve'pi ca kvacidvākye brahmaśabdaḥ prayujyate |
tadīyakarmahetutvājjanmādestajjalāniti || 125 ||
hetūktirapi yuktā syādityetattu na sanmatam |
sarvavastunyātmatayā proktaṃ brahma paraḥ pumān || 126 ||
sthalāntare prasiddhasya tasyaivātrānuvādataḥ |
tajjatvādīnanūdyoktā taddhetukatadātmatā || 127 ||
jīvātmanaḥ karmakṛtadevamartyādirūpiṇaḥ |
na syādvedāntavākyeṣu yasmājjanmādayaśśrutāḥ || 128 ||
sa evātra brahmaśabdavācyassyātsa paraḥ pumān |
brahmaṇaścidacidvastuśarīratvāccharīriṇi || 129 ||
doṣāśśarīragā na syurato nirduṣṭa eva saḥ |
jīvasya sarvatādātmyamuktaṃ tannopapadyate || 130 ||
tattaccharīrabhinnānāṃ tādātmyāsaṃbhavānmithaḥ |
ato'tra paramātmaiva brahmaśabdārtha iṣyate || 131 ||
|| vivakṣitaguṇopapatteśca || 
manomayatvādayaśca guṇāssarve vivakṣitāḥ |
para evopapadyante satyasaṃkalpatānvitāḥ || 132 ||
anupapattestu na śārīraḥ ||
eṣāṃ guṇānāmetasmin śārīre'nupapattitaḥ |
na śārīro'yamapi tu paraṃ brahmātra gīyate || 133 ||
|| karmakartṛvyapadeśācca || 
abhisaṃbhavitāsmīti dhyeyaḥ prāpyatayocyate |
jīvaḥ prāptṛtayokto'to jīvādanyaḥ paraḥ pumān || 134 ||
|| śabdaviśeṣāt || 
vākya eṣa ma ātmeti ṣaṣṭhyā śārīra ucyate |
upāsyastu prathamayā tasmādanyaḥ paraḥ pumān || 135 ||
|| smṛteśca || 
sarvasya cāhamityādismṛtirjīvamupāsakam |
parātmānamupāsyaṃ ca vakti jīvetarastataḥ || 136 ||
|| arbhakaukastvāttadvyapadeśācca neti cenna nicāyyatvādevaṃ
vyomavacca || 
nālpatvālpasthānavatvavyapadeśādayaṃ paraḥ |
iti cennopadeśo'yaṃ tathopāsyatvahetukaḥ || 137 ||
na svarūpālpatāsūcī jyāyastvavyapadeśataḥ |
|| saṃbhogaprāptiriti cenna vaiśeṣyāt || 
upāsakaśarīre'sau parātmā vartate yadi || 138 ||
sukhaduḥkhopabhogasya prāptistadvadamuṣya ca |
iti cenna śarīrāntaḥsthatvameva na kāraṇam || 139 ||
karmavaśyatvamevopabhoge heturna tatpare |
heyapratibhaṭe sarvakalyāṇaguṇasāgare || 140 ||
|| iti sarvatraprasiddhyadhikaraṇam ||
|| attradhikaraṇam || 
|| attā carācaragrahaṇāt || 
paro yadi na bhoktā syāttarhi bhoktṛtayā śrutaḥ |
sarvatra jīva eva syāditi vādo'tra vāryate || 141 ||
yasya brahmetyādimantre kaṭhavallīgate śrutaḥ |
attā jīvo'syaiva karmaphalabhoktṛtvasaṃbhavāt || 142 ||
maivamattā parātmaiva viśvāttṛtvaśruteriha |
mṛtyūpasecanāddhyatra kṛtsnasyādanamucyate || 143 ||
īdṛśaṃ cādanaṃ sarvasaṃhāro'tra vivakṣitaḥ |
karmopādhikabhoktṛtvaṃ nedaṃ kiṃ tu parātmanaḥ || 144 ||
upasaṃhartṛtvamidaṃ bhoktṛtvaṃ jagatāṃ tataḥ |
jīve'saṃbhāvitamidaṃ tasmādattā paraḥ pumān || 145 ||
|| prakaraṇācca || 
mahāntaṃ vibhumātmānaṃ nāyamātmeti ca sthitam |
parasyedaṃ prakaraṇaṃ tasmāccāyaṃ paraḥ pumān || 146 ||
guhāṃ praviṣṭāvātmānau hi taddarśanāt || 
ṛtaṃ pibantāvityatra phalabhoktṛtayā śrutaḥ |
saprāṇo jīva evātaścātrāpyattā sa ityasat || 147 ||
guhāṃ praviṣṭāvucyete atra jīvaparāvubhau |
tatpraveśastayoreva sthale'sminkila dṛśyate || 148 ||
taṃ durdaśamiti śrutyā parasyoktā guhāsthitiḥ |
jīvasya tatsthitistvatra yā prāṇeneti vākyataḥ || 149 ||
ṛtaṃ pibantāvityuktiścchatrinyāyena saṃgatā |
parasya pāne yogassyātprayojakatayāpi vā || 150 ||
|| viśeṣaṇācca || 
ādito'sminprakaraṇa āntaṃ jīvaparāvubhau |
tattaddharmairviśiṣyete tasmādattā paraḥ pumān || 151 ||
|| ityattradhikaraṇam ||
|| antarādhikaraṇam ||
|| antara upapatteḥ || 
durjñeyatvaṃ parasyoktaṃ ka itthetyādinā purā |
tarhi dṛśyatayokto na para ityetadasyate || 152 ||
akṣyādhāraḥ śrutassāmni ya eṣa iti vākyataḥ |
jīvo vā pratibimbo vā'thākṣyadhiṣṭhātṛdevatā || 153 ||
prasiddhavadvacanata eṣvaivānyatamo bhavet |
parātmanastvakṣṇi dṛśyatvāprasiddhyā sa nocyate || 154 ||
maivaṃ paro'kṣyantarasthastasminnevopapattitaḥ |
saṃyadvāmatvādinityanisargaguṇasampadām || 155 ||
|| sthānādivyapadeśācca || 
akṣṇi sthitiniyantrṛtvādyasyaiva paramātmanaḥ |
yaścakṣuṣītyevamādau śrūyate'to'kṣigaḥ paraḥ || 156 ||
prasiddhavadvaco'taśca parātmanyeva yujyate |
atra dṛśyata ityuktiryogidṛśyatvahetukā || 157 ||
|| sukhaviśiṣṭābhidhānādeva ca || 
sukhavaiśiṣṭyavatvena kaṃ brahmetyādinā purā |
prakṛtasya parasyaivopāstisthānādisiddhaye || 158 ||
ya eṣa iti nirdeśādatrākṣipuruṣaḥ paraḥ |
|| ata eva ca sa brahma || 
kaṃ brahmetyādinā proktaṃ kathaṃ brahmeti kathyate || 159 ||
ke khe caiva brahmadṛṣṭividhissyāditi cenna tat |
yadvāva kaṃ tadeveti yatastatra khavastunaḥ || 160 ||
sukhavaiśiṣṭyopadeśo'ta evātra khaśabditaḥ |
ākāśasya paraṃbrahmāparicchinnasukhātmakam || 161 ||
prāṇaṃ cāsmai tadākāśaṃ cocurityuktamantataḥ |
tasmātprāk prakṛtaṃ brahmāparicchinnasukhātmakam || 162 ||
akṣyādhāratayātrāpi śrūyate'to'kṣigaḥ paraḥ |
|| śrutopaniṣatkagatyabhidhānācca || 
vijñātabrahmayāthātmyairgantavyatvena yā gatiḥ || 163 ||
brahmāptaye śrutā'nyatra sārcirādigatistviha |
śrutākṣipuruṣasyābhigantavyatvena paṭhyate || 164 ||
atopyasāvakṣivartī paramātmeti niścitaḥ |
|| anavasthiterasaṃbhavācca netaraḥ || 
jīvasya pratibimbasyādityasyāpyanavasthiteḥ || 165 ||
niyamenākṣiṇi tathā nirupādhikatājuṣām |
asaṃbhavādguṇānāṃ ca parātmaivākṣigaḥ pumān || 166 ||
|| ityantarādhikaraṇam ||
|| antaryāmyadhikaraṇam ||
|| antaryāmyadhidaivādhilokādiṣu taddharmavyapadeśāt || 
cakṣussthitiṃ parasya prāk niścityārthaḥ prasādhitaḥ |
jīvasyaiva sthitisseti vādo'tra vinivāryate || 167 ||
antaryāmibrāhyaṇe yaḥ pṛthivyāmiti vākyataḥ |
antaryāmī śruto yo'sau jīvostu draṣṭṛtādibhiḥ || 168 ||
draṣṭrantaraniṣedhāccetyevaṃ vādo na saṃmataḥ |
adhidaivādhilokādipadāṅkitavacaśśrutaḥ || 169 ||
antaryāmī parātmaiva taddharmavyapadeśataḥ |
jagaccharīrakatvaṃ ca sarvāntaryāmitā tathā || 170 ||
atraivamādyāḥ paṭhitā dharmā nārāyaṇāśritāḥ |
darśanādi tu rūpādisākṣātkāro na cānyathā || 171 ||
nānyo'tostītyadovākyaṃ tulyadraṣṭṭaniṣedhakam |
|| na ca smārtamataddharmābhilāpācchārīraśca || 
pradhānaṃ ca tathā jīvo nāntaryāmī tayordvayoḥ || 172 ||
asaṃbhāvitadharmāṇāmabhilāpādanekaśaḥ |
acetanaṃ yathā nārhameteṣāṃ cetanastathā || 173 ||
|| ubhaye'pi hi bhedenainamadhīyate || 
kāṇvā mādhyandināścaiva yo vijñāne ya ātmani |
ityantaryāmiṇaṃ jīvāt bhedenādhīyate yataḥ || 174 ||
jīvādvilakṣaṇo'to'sāvantaryāmī paraḥ pumān |
dhvastapāpmā parātmā sa nārāyaṇa iti sthitam || 175 ||
|| ityantaryāmyadhikaraṇam ||
|| adṛśyatvādiguṇakādhikaraṇam ||
|| adṛśyatvādiguṇako dharmokteḥ || 
uktaṃ draṣṭṭatvādinā prāk parātmatvaṃ na yatra tat |
tatra pradhānameva syāditi śaṅkā vyudasyate || 176 ||
yattadadreśyamityādau śrūyate muṇḍakaśrutau |
yadbhūtayonimiti cāthā kṣarātparataḥ paraḥ || 177 ||
sthale'trājājīvaghanāvevocyete yathākramam |
pratiṣedhādacidvartidṛśyatvāderacetanam || 178 ||
sajātīyaṃ pradhānaṃ syāt bhūtayonyakṣaraṃ tathā |
parataścākṣarājjīvaghanassyātpara ityasat || 179 ||
atrādṛśyatvādiguṇaścākṣarātparataḥ paraḥ |
paramātmaiva taddharmasarvajñatvādisaṃśruteḥ || 180 ||
pañcamyantākṣarapadaṃ bhūtasūkṣmaṃ bravīti tat |
nādṛśyatvādiguṇakamakṣaraṃ tasya sarvataḥ || 181 ||
paratvena tatonyasya paratvāsaṃbhavādidam |
sarvajñatvajagadyonitvādibhirhi viśeṣyate || 182 ||
|| viśeṣaṇabhedavyapadeśābhyāṃ ca netarau || 
mahāprakaraṇaṃ cātra pradhānātpuruṣādapi |
sarvajñānapratijñādyairvyāvartayati cākṣaram || 183 ||
parataḥ para ityābhyāṃ bhedaścāsyopadiśyate |
tasmādatrocyate tābhyāṃ paro nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 184 ||
|| rūpopanyāsācca || 
agnirmūrdhe tyādinoktaṃ rūpaṃ trailokyarūpiṇaḥ |
paramātmana eva syādato'yaṃ paramaḥ pumān || 185 ||
|| ityadṛśyatvādiguṇakādhikaraṇam ||
vaiśvānarādhikaraṇam ||
|| vaiśvānarassādhāraṇaśabdaviśeṣāt || 
trailokyamūrtimattvena parātmatvaṃ samarthitam |
tat tadanyasyāpi cāstītyāśaṅkātra vyudasyate || 186 ||
sāmānyātmānamevemaṃ vaiśvānaramiti sthale |
vaiśvānaraḥ parātmeti nirṇetuṃ naiva śakyate || 187 ||
jāṭharāgnau tatpadasya bhūte deve parātmani |
caturṣvapi prayogādatraiṣāṃ liṅgopalabdhitaḥ || 188 ||
maivaṃ vaiśvānarapadavācyaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ |
sādhāraṇaitacchabdasya parāsādhāraṇairguṇaiḥ || 189 ||
viśeṣyamāṇatvādatrānekaissarvātmatādibhiḥ |
|| smaryamāṇamanumānaṃ syāditi || 
dyumūrdhatvādimadrūpamuktaṃ vaiśvānarātmanaḥ || 190 ||
śrutismṛtiprasiddhaṃ tat tadevedamitīha ca |
pratyabhijñāyamānaṃ sadasya vaiśvānarātmanaḥ || 191 ||
parātmatve liṅgametadato vaiśvānaraḥ paraḥ |
|| śabdādibhyo'ntaḥ pratiṣṭhānācca neti cenna tathā dṛṣṭyupadeśādasaṃbhavātpuruṣamapi cainamadhīyate || 
agnirvaiśvānara iti sāmānādhikaraṇyataḥ || 192 ||
agnitretākalpanācca sthānātprāṇāhutestathā |
śarīrāntaḥ sthiteścāsya jāṭharatvaṃ pratīyate || 193 ||
tasmādasya parātmatvaṃ neti cettanna tādṛśaḥ |
vaiśvānarasyodaryāgniśarīrakatayā'tra ca || 194 ||
tadviśiṣṭopāsanasya vidhānātkevalasya tu |
jāṭharasya trilokāṅgakatvāsaṃbhavatastathā || 195 ||
evaṃ vaiśvānarātmānameke vājasaneyinaḥ |
puruṣaṃ cādhīyate sa eṣogniriti vākyataḥ || 196 ||
nirgatopādhipuruṣaśabdavācyatvamasya vai |
ato vaiśvānarātmaivopāsyo nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 197 ||
|| ata eva na devatā bhūtaṃ ca || 
yato vaiśvānarātmā'yaṃ puruṣo nirupādhikaḥ |
trailokyāṅgaścāta eva na bhūtaṃ na ca devatā || 198 ||
|| sākṣādapyavirodhaṃ jaiminiḥ || 
agniśabdopyatra sākṣādagraprāpakatāguṇāt |
paramātmānamācaṣṭe vaiśvānarapadaṃ yathā || 199 ||
ato vaiśvānarasyāgniśabditatvaṃ na bādhitam |
paratvanirṇaye'treti jaiminirmanyate muniḥ || 200 ||
|| abhivyakterityāśmarathyaḥ || 
upāsakābhivyaktyarthaṃ vimānasya parātmanaḥ |
paricchinnatvopadeśa ityāhātrā''śmarathyakaḥ || 201 ||
|| anusmṛterbādariḥ || 
mūrdhaprabhṛtipādāntadehakalpanamatra yat |
tathopāstyarthamityāha bādarirbhagavānṛṣiḥ || 202 ||
|| saṃpatteriti jaiministathāhi darśayati || 
upāsakasya cāṅgānāṃ gārhapatyādikalpanam |
pratyahaṃ kriyamāṇasya prāṇāhutyākhyakarmaṇaḥ || 203 ||
agnihotratvasaṃpattyai iti jaiminirabravīt |
ya etadevamiti ca saṃpatiṃta darśayatyamūm || 204 ||
|| āmananti cainamasmin || 
upāsakaśarīre'sminnenaṃ dyubhvādidehakam |
vaiśvānaraṃ parātmānaṃ prāṇāgnihavanena ca || 205 ||
ārādhyatvāyāmananti vāca stasyetyupakramāḥ |
ato nārāyaṇa iti siddhaṃ vaiśvānaraḥ pumān || 206 ||
|| iti vaiśvānarādhikaraṇam ||
|| iti prathamādhyāye dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| atha prathamādhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| dyubhvādyadhikaraṇam ||
|| dyubhvādyāyatanaṃ svaśabdāt || 
prāgaspaṣṭatarāspaṣṭaliṅgavākyaṃ vicāritam |
pāde'tra spaṣṭajīvādiliṅgavākyaṃ vicāryate || 207 ||
dyupṛthivyādyāśrayasya parātmatvaṃ samarthitam |
tadevātrādhikaraṇa ākṣipya sthāpyate dṛḍham || 208 ||
yasmindyauḥ pṛthivīceti muṇḍopaniṣadi śrutaḥ |
jīva evātra karaṇāśrayatvaṃ bahudhā janiḥ || 209 ||
nāḍīyogaścaivamādijīvadharmacayaśruteḥ |
maivaṃ dyubhvādyāyatanaṃ paraṃ brahma svaśabdataḥ || 210 ||
svaśabdo'trāmṛtasyaiṣa seturityevamādikaḥ |
amṛtatvaprāptihetussa eveti nigadyate || 211 ||
ātmaśabdaścātra pare vartate nirupādhikaḥ |
sarvajñatvādayo'troktāstasminneva susaṅgatāḥ || 212 ||
nāḍyāśrayatvaṃ cāsyoktaṃ santataśrutivākyataḥ |
ajāyamāna ityādiśrutyā'jopīti ca smṛteḥ || 213 ||
bahudhā jāyamānatvaṃ siddhamasya nijecchayā |
manaḥprabhṛtyāśrayatvaṃ sarvādharasya yujyate || 214 ||
|| muktopasṛpyavyapadeśācca || 
tadā vidvānpuṇyapāpe ityādiśrutivākyataḥ |
bandhānmuktaiḥ prāpyatayā nirdeśācca paraḥ pumān || 215 ||
|| nānumānamatacchabdātprāṇabhṛcca || 
nātra pradhānaṃ tadvāciśabdābhāvādyathocyate |
tathā prāṇabhṛdapyeṣa pratipādyo na vākyataḥ || 216 ||
|| bhedavyapadeśāt || 
samāne vṛkṣa ityādivākyena vyapadiśyate |
jīvādvilakṣaṇatvena mahimārṇava īśvaraḥ || 217 ||
|| prakaraṇāt || 
parasyaivātrā'tha parā yayeti śrutivāgbharaiḥ |
prakṛtatvātparaṃ brahmaivaitatprakaraṇaśrutam || 218 ||
|| sthityadanābhyāṃ ca || 
dvāsuparṇetyādimantre phalabhoktṛtvamucyate |
ekasyānyasya dehāntassthitimātraṃ pradarśyate || 219 ||
tatrā'naśnandīpyamāno dyubhvādyāyatanaṃ bhavet |
na tadbhoktā tato dyubhvādyayanaḥ paramaḥ pumān || 220 ||
|| iti dyubhvādyadhikaraṇam ||
|| bhūmādhikaraṇam || 
|| bhūmā saṃprasādādadhyupadeśāt || 
parātmatvaṃ prakaraṇāvicchedātprāk samarthitam |
taddhetoreva bhūmātra jīva ityetadasyate || 221 ||
yatra nānyatpaśyatīti vākyaṃ sāmani paṭhyate |
tatra bhūmā pratyagātmā tasminprāṇapadodite || 222 ||
nāmādyātmopadeśasya samāptestadanantaram |
praśnapratyuttarābhāvātprāṇaprakaraṇasya ca || 223 ||
avicchedādatra bhūmā pratyagātmeti cenna tat |
bhūmā paraḥ saṃprasādādadhikatvopadeśataḥ || 224 ||
saṃprasādaḥ pratyagātmetyanyaśrutyā'vasīyate |
svopāsyādhikyavāditvamuktvā'sya prāṇavedinaḥ || 225 ||
eṣatviti tuśabdenāthātivādyantaraṃ svayam |
upakṣipyāsya satyopāsituḥ prāṇātivāditaḥ || 226 ||
ādhikyokteśca vicchedātprāṇaprakaraṇasya ca |
prāṇādarthāntaratvena proktaḥ satyapadoditaḥ || 227 ||
atyantasukharūpo'sau bhūmā nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ |
|| dharmopapatteśca || 
svābhāvikāmṛtatvaṃ ca sve mahimnyeva saṃsthitiḥ || 228 ||
sarvātmatvaṃ caivamādidharmā ye bhūmani śrutāḥ |
teṣāṃ parasminnevopapatterbhūmā paraḥ pumān || 229 ||
|| iti bhūmādhikaraṇam ||
|| akṣarādhikaraṇam || 
|| akṣaramambarāntadhṛteḥ || 
sve mahimnisthitatvaṃ prāk brahmaṇaḥ pratipāditam |
tadanyasminnapi parādastītyetannivāryate || 230 ||
gārgipraśne sa hovācaitadvā ityādi paṭhyate |
atrākṣaraṃ pradhānaṃ syādasthūlatvādidharmavat || 231 ||
pradhāne'kṣaraśabdasya prayogādakṣarāditi |
sarvakāryādhārabhūtasyākāśasyāpi kāraṇe || 232 ||
pṛṣṭe pratyucyamānaṃ tatsyādavyākṛtamakṣaram |
maivamatrākṣarapadavācyaḥ paramapūrūṣaḥ || 233 ||
ākāśapārabhūtasya pradhānasyāpi dhāraṇāt |
yadūrdhvamiti cārabhya kālatritayavartinaḥ || 234 ||
vikārajātasyādhāratayā proktaṃ na vāyumat |
ambaraṃ kintu tatpārabhūtamavyākṛtaṃ bhavet || 235 ||
tadādhāratayoktaṃ tadakṣaraṃ na pradhānakam |
|| sā ca praśāsanāt || 
tarhi jīvostvakṣaraṃ tatkṛtsnācetanavastunaḥ || 236 ||
ādhāratvopapatterityeṣa vādo nivāryate |
etasya vā akṣarasyetyādinā'syākṣarasya sā || 237 ||
dhṛtiḥ praśāsanādeva bhavatītyupadiśyate |
na cedṛk śāsanaṃ baddhamuktayorapi saṃbhavet || 238 ||
|| anyabhāvavyavṛtteśca || 
adṛṣṭaṃ draṣṭṭa cetyādivākyaśeṣaḥ parātmataḥ |
anyatvamakṣarasyāsya vyāvartayati copari || 239 ||
īdṛgguṇaviśiṣṭānyasadbhāvaṃ vā vyudasyati |
tasmātsvaśāsanādhīnasarvadhartā paraḥ pumān || 240 ||
|| ityakṣarādhikaraṇam ||
|| īkṣatikarmādhikaraṇam || 
|| īkṣatikarma vyapadeśātsaḥ || 
parasyānyairdarśanārhatvābhāvaḥ prāk samīritaḥ |
tarhi dṛśyatayokto na parassyāditi vāryate || 241 ||
praśne yaḥ punaretaṃ trimātreṇetyādi paṭhyate |
paraṃ puruṣaṃ ityevaṃ nirdiṣṭo'tra caturmukhaḥ || 242 ||
ekadvimātrapraṇavamantropāsīnayoḥ kramāt |
martyalokāptyantarikṣalokaprāptyātmake phale || 243 ||
uktvā trimātropāsīnaprāpyatvena samīritaḥ |
antarikṣātparo brahmalokastatsāhacaryataḥ || 244 ||
viriñcalokastatrasthenekṣyamāṇassa eva hi |
maivamīkṣatikarmātra paraḥ puruṣa eva saḥ || 245 ||
parādityādivākyāntyaśloka īkṣatikarmaṇaḥ |
nirupādhikaśāntatvāmṛtatvādyupadeśataḥ || 246 ||
evaṃ siddhe parātmatve brahmalokapadaṃ ca tat |
yattatkavaya ityevamatroktaṃ sūrisevitam || 247 ||
tadvaikuṇṭhasthānameva vadatītyavagamyate |
antarikṣāvyavahito na ca lokopi vedhasaḥ || 248 ||
madhye svargādilokānāṃ bahūnāṃ saṃbhavādataḥ |
atrekṣateḥ karmatayā śruto nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 249 ||
|| iti īkṣatikarmādhikaraṇam ||
|| daharādhikaraṇam || 
|| dahara uttarebhyaḥ || 
pūrvaṃ puriśayānasya parātmatvaṃ samarthitam |
tasyākāśapadoktasya sthāpyate'tra parātmatā || 250 ||
chāndogye cā tha yadidamasminnatyādi paṭhyate |
śruto'tra daharākāśo bhūtākāśo'vasīyate || 251 ||
tatraivākāśaśabdasya prakarṣeṇa prasiddhitaḥ |
anveṣṭavyādhāratayā pratīteśceti cenna tat || 252 ||
dahrākāśaḥ parātmā'trottaravākyoktadharmataḥ |
svābhāvikātmatvamapahatapāpmatvapyatha || 253 ||
satyasaṅkalpatā cātha satyakāmatvamityapi |
parātmaikāntadharmāste paṭhitā vākya uttare || 254 ||
anveṣṭavyāntaraṃ cātra tadantassthaguṇāṣṭakam |
dahraṃ ca guṇajātaṃ ca prastutyātha dvayorapi || 255 ||
tadanveṣṭavyamityevamupāsyatvaṃ vidhīyate |
yāvānvā ayamākāśastāvāni tyādivākyataḥ || 256 ||
dahrabhūtākāśayośca spaṣṭaṃ bhedaḥ pratīyate |
ataśca daharākāśaḥ paramātmā na khaṃ bhavet || 257 ||
|| gatiśabdābhyāṃ tathāhi dṛṣṭaṃ liṅgaṃ ca ||
asmiṃśca daharākāśe cetanānāmajānatām |
pratyahaṃ gamanaṃ cātha gantavyadaharasya ca || 258 ||
brahmalokapadoktiścetyubhayaṃ paramātmatām |
bodhayatyasya dahrasya dṛṣṭamanyatra taddvayam || 259 ||
sati saṃpadya netyeṣa brahmaloka iti śruteḥ |
kimanyadṛṣṭyā syādatra śrūyamāṇamidaṃ dvayam || 260 ||
dahrasya paramātmatve liṅgaṃ paryāptameva hi |
|| dhṛteśca mahimno'syāsminnupalabdheḥ || 
brahmaikāntatayā sarvavedāntodghoṣitasya ca || 261 ||
jagaddhṛtyākhyabhūmno'smin dahre saṃkīrtanādapi |
dahrākāśaḥ paraḥ śrīmān nārāyaṇa itīryate || 262 ||
|| prasiddheśca || 
ko hyevānyātka ityādiṣvasyākāśapadasya ca |
parātmani prasiddheśca dahrākāśaḥ paraḥ pumān || 263 ||
|| itaraparāmarśātsa iti cennāsaṃbhavāt || 
ya eṣa saṃprasādo'smāditi jīvaḥ paretaraḥ |
prokta evātra daharākāśassyāditi cenna tat || 264 ||
asminnasaṃbhavāduktaguṇānāṃ dahravartinām |
|| uttarāccedāvirbhūtasvarūpastu || 
prajāpateruttaratra vākye jīvopi tādṛśaḥ || 265 ||
dhvastapāpmatvādinoktastasmāddahrassa ityasat |
tirohitaguṇaḥ pūrvaṃ brahmopāsanayā tataḥ || 266 ||
āvirbhūtasvarūpastu tatra jīvaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
svābhāvikaguṇo dahrastvatrāto nātra jīvadhīḥ || 267 ||
|| anyārthaśca parāmarśaḥ || 
jīvasvarūpāvirbhāvāpādanākhyāgryasaṃpadaḥ |
parasmin sūcanāyātra jīvasya grahaṇaṃ kṛtam || 268 ||
|| alpaśruteriti cettaduktam || 
alpamānaśrutistvatra jīvasyaivopapadyate |
jyāyaso neti cedatra pūrvamevoktamuttaram || 169 ||
|| anukṛtestasya ca || 
parānukārānmuktasya guṇāṣṭakaviśiṣṭatā |
ato'nukarturjīvāccā'nukāryo daharaḥ paraḥ || 270 ||
|| api smaryate || 
paropāstyā'sya jīvasya tatsāmyāpattirucyate |
idaṃ jñānamiti smṛtyāṃ tahro jīvetaraḥ paraḥ || 271 ||
|| iti daharādhikaraṇam ||
|| pramitādhikaraṇam || 
|| śabdādeva pramitaḥ || 
prāgalpaparimāṇatvaṃ parātmana udāhṛtam |
tasyaivātrāṅguṣṭhaparimāṇavatvaṃ nigadyate || 272 ||
aṅguṣṭhamātra ityādyāḥ kaṭhavallīṣvṛcaḥ śrutāḥ |
tatrāṅguṣṭhapramāṇo'yaṃ jīva eveti niścayaḥ || 273 ||
prāṇādhipo'ṅguṣṭhamātra ityādiśrutivākyataḥ |
jīvātmano'ṅguṣṭhaparimāṇavatvāvabodhanāt || 274 ||
maivamatrāṅguṣṭhamānapramitaḥ paramaḥ pumān |
īśāno bhūtabhavyasyetyetadekāntaśabdataḥ || 275 ||
|| hṛdyapekṣayā tu manuṣyādhikāratvāt || 
upāsakahṛdo'ṅguṣṭhapramāṇatvātparātmanaḥ |
tatra sthitestadaṅguṣṭhamānatvaṃ tadapekṣayā || 276 ||
śāstrasya tu manuṣyādhikāratvāttadapekṣayā |
parasyāṅguṣṭhamānoktiryuktiranyāpi vakṣyate || 277 ||
|| madhye devatādhikaraṇam ||
|| taduparyapi bādarāyaṇassaṃbhavāt ||
brahmopāsanaśāsraṃ prākproktaṃ martyādhikārakam |
devādīnāmasti na vetyadhikāro'tra cintyate || 278 ||
nopāstāvadhikāro'sti devādīnāmadehinām |
dehinirvanīye'tropāsane na samarthatā || 279 ||
maivaṃ paropāsanaṃ tadamareṣvapi saṃbhavet |
ityevaṃ manyate vyāso bhagavānbādarāyaṇaḥ || 280 ||
teṣāmarthitvasāmarthyasaṃbhavādarthitā ca sā |
paramātmani kalyāṇaguṇābdhau bhogyatādhiyā || 281 ||
bhavatyathaiṣāṃ sāmarthyamapi saṃbhavati svayam |
dehendriyādimattvena tadvattvaṃ sarvanākinām || 282 ||
vedānte śrūyate sṛṣṭisthale copāsanasthale |
tasmātteṣāṃ tayossattvādadhikārostyupāsane || 283 ||
|| virodhaḥ karmaṇīti cennānekapratipatterdarśanāt || 
dehavattvepi devānāṃ virodhaḥ syāddhi karmaṇi |
bahuyāgeṣu yugapadāhūtasyaikanākinaḥ || 284 ||
sānnidhyāsaṃbhavātsarvatreti cenna svaśaktitaḥ |
saubharyādau dṛśyate'yaṃ naikadehaparigrahaḥ || 285 ||
|| śabda iti cennātaḥ prabhavātpratyakṣānumānābhyām || 
virodho vaidike śabde syādanityārthayogataḥ |
indrādidevadehasyānityatvāttasya janmanaḥ || 286 ||
prāgvināśātparaṃ caiṣāṃ śabdānāṃ vedavartinām |
arthaśūnyatvamathavā'nityatvamiti cenna tat || 287 ||
indrādiśabdādevendrādyarthasya prabhavātpunaḥ |
nendrādyā vaidikāśśabdāḥ pravṛttā vyaktimātrake || 288 ||
gavādiśabdavattatrākṛtivācitayā tataḥ |
ekavyaktau vinaṣṭāyāṃ tattadvaidikaśabdataḥ || 289 ||
manassthitāccāvagatatattadarthaḥ prajāpatiḥ |
anyaṃ tadākṛtiṃ cendraṃ sṛjatītyavagamyate || 290 ||
śrutismṛtibhyāṃ tasmānna virodhaśśabdagocaraḥ |
|| ata eva ca nityatvam || 
yato dhātā vaidikācca śabdājjātārthasaṃsmṛtiḥ || 291 ||
mantrādikṛdṛṣīṇāmākṛtiśaktyādikaṃ punaḥ |
parāmṛśya tadākāraśaktiyuktān tadā tadā || 292 ||
sṛṣṭvā tattanmantrakṛtau tāneva prerayatyataḥ |
vedanityatvameṣāṃ ca mantrakṛttvamapi sthitam || 293 ||
|| samānanāmarūpatvāccāvṛttāvapyavirodho darśanātsmṛteśca || 
kṛtsnanāśe jagatsṛṣṭyāvṛttāvapyuktarītitaḥ |
samānanāmarūpatvādavirodho'vagamyatām || 294 ||
sa punarbhagavān sākṣāt brahmāṇḍaṃ ca caturmukham |
sṛṣṭvā yathāpūrvameva sarvavedān svahṛddhṛtān || 295 ||
caturmukhāyopadiśya sṛṣṭikārye ca pūrvavat |
taṃ niyujya svayaṃ cāpi niyantṛtvena vartate || 296 ||
pūrvānupūrvīṃ tāmeva smṛtvā saṃskārato dṛḍhāt |
tayaivoccāryamāṇatvaṃ vedasyāpauruṣeyatā || 297 ||
nityatā cedṛśaścārthaḥ śrutismṛtyavabodhitaḥ |
devādīnāmato'styevādhikriyā brahmavedane || 298 ||
|| iti devatādhikaraṇam ||
|| atha madhvadhikaraṇam || 
|| madhvādiṣvasaṃbhavādanadhikāraṃ jaiminiḥ || 
adhikāro'sti devānāṃ brahmavidyāsvitīritam |
tadasaṃbhavaśaṅkā ca tadviśeṣe nirasyate || 299 ||
vasvādityādidevānāṃ prāptānāṃ vasutādikam |
tatprāpakeṣu madhvādiṣvarthitāderasaṃbhavāt || 300 ||
adhikārābhāvamatra manyate jaiminirguruḥ |
ye yāsu vidyāsūpāsyā nahyatraiṣāmadhikriyā || 301 ||
|| jyotiṣi bhāvācca || 
brahmaṇyupastiruktātra taṃ devā iti nākinām |
sādhāraṇyena siddhāyāṃ tasyāṃ vibudhamartyayoḥ || 302 ||
viśiṣya śravaṇaṃ tveṣāṃ brahmānyopāstyanuṣṭhitim |
nivārayatyatasteṣāṃ madhvādiṣvanadhikriyā || 303 ||
|| bhāvaṃ tu bādarāyaṇo'sti hi || 
vasvādīnāṃ ca madhvādau bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ |
manyate'dhikriyāyogaṃ vasvādaya ime surāḥ || 304 ||
kalpāntarepi saṃprāpya madhye vasvāditāpadam |
ante brahmaprāptukāmā madhuvidyātmikāmimām || 305 ||
brahmavidyāmadhiṣṭhāya svāvasthaṃ brahma nityaśaḥ |
arhantyupāsituṃ tasmātteṣāmatrāstyadhikriyā || 306 ||
|| iti madhvadhikaraṇam ||
|| atha apaśūdrādhikaraṇam || 
|| śugasya tadanādaraśravaṇāttadā''dravaṇātsūcyate hi || 
brahmavidyādhikriyoktā devānāṃ yogyatānvayāt |
śūdrasyāpyadhikāro'stu tatsatvāditi vāryate || 307 ||
śūdrasyāpyadhikāro'tra brahmavidyāsu vidyate |
śrautadhīvirahepyasya purāṇaśravaṇādinā || 308 ||
brahmasvarūpataddhyānaprakārajñānasaṃbhavāt |
mumukṣutāsaṃbhavācca sāmarthyārthitvasaṃbhavāt || 309 ||
tasmājjānaśrutiṃ raikvaśśūdretyāmantrya tasya hi |
upādiśadbrahmavidyāmiti vādo'samañjasaḥ || 310 ||
jānaśruterhaṃsanindāvākyānuśravaṇādgurum |
tadaiva raikvaṃ pratyevādravaṇātsūcyate'sya śuk || 311 ||
tāmuddiśyaiva śūdreti gurūktirnatu jātitaḥ |
vedāntavākyajanitavijñānasyaiva vedane || 312 ||
upāyatvaśrutestasya śūdrajātāvasaṃbhavāt |
arthitvasaṃbhavepyasya sāmarthyaṃ tu na vidyate || 313 ||
purāṇaśravaṇānujñā kṛtā pāpapraśāntaye |
śūdrasyātaḥ paropāstau sarvathā nāstyadhikriyā || 314 ||
|| kṣatriyatvagateśca || 
jānaśruteśca śuśrūṣorbahudāyīti vākyataḥ |
kṣatriyatvapratīteśca tadāhvānaṃ na jātitaḥ || 315 ||
|| uttaratra caitrarathena liṅgāt || 
asyāṃ saṃvargavidyāyāmuttarākhyāyikāsthale |
kṣatriyasyaiva sambandho brāhmaṇetarajātiṣu || 316 ||
abhipratāriṇaścaitrarathākhyasya pratīyate |
jānaśrutirataścātra raikvānyaḥ kṣatriyo mataḥ || 317 ||
kāpeyasāhacaryācca jñāyate'bhipratāriṇaḥ |
anyatra darśanāccaitrarathatā rājatāpi ca || 318 ||
|| saṃskāraparāmarśāttadabhāvābhilāpāśca || 
vidyopadeśavākyeṣu saṃskārasyābhimarśanāt |
śūdrasya tadabhāvācca nādhikāro'tra vidyate || 319 ||
|| tadabhāvanirdhāraṇe ca pravṛtteḥ || 
jābālasya ca śuśrūṣośśūdratvābhāvaniccaye |
sthite satyevopadeśapravṛtternāsya yogyatā || 320 ||
|| śravaṇādhyayanārthapratiṣedhāt || 
śūdrasya vedaśravaṇādhyayanādīni sarvathā |
niṣidhyante tatastasya nādhikāro'styupāsane || 321 ||
|| smṛteśca || 
na cāsyopadiśeddharmamityādismṛtivākyataḥ |
śravaṇādiniṣedhaśca spaṣṭo nāto'sya yogyatā || 322 ||
|| ityapaśūdrādhikaraṇam ||
|| atha pramitādhikaraṇaśeṣaḥ ||
|| kampanāt || 
prāsaṅgikaṃ samāpyātha prakṛtasya parātmatām |
aṅguṣṭhapramitasyaiva vaktuṃ yuktiḥ pradarśyate || 323 ||
kaṭhe'tra yadidaṃ kiñcetyādinābhihitādbhayāt |
aṅguṣṭhapramitaprāṇaśabdavācyotthitātsadā || 324 ||
vajrādivodyatātsarvadevānāṃ kampanādbhṛśam |
aṅguṣṭhapramitastādṛksvabhāvaḥ paramaḥ pumān || 325 ||
|| jyotirdarśanāt || 
na tatra sūrya ityādivākyoktasya sthale'tra ca |
sarvabhāsāṃ kāraṇasya parāsādhāraṇasya ca || 326 ||
jyotiṣo darśanānnityanirgatātiśayasya ca |
aṅguṣṭhapramitaḥ śrīmān nārāyaṇa itīryate || 327 ||
|| ityaṅguṣṭhapramitādhikaraṇam ||
|| arthāntaratvādivyapadeśādhikaraṇam || 
|| ākāśo'rthāntaratvādivyapadeśāt || 
sāmanyākāśaśabdasya śrutasya daharāntike |
āśaṅkyārtho jīva iti paramātmeti sādhyate || 328 ||
sāmnyākāśo havai nāmarūpayoriti paṭhyate |
atrākāśo muktajīvastasyaiva prastutatvataḥ || 329 ||
dhūtvetyuttaravākye cetyevaṃ vādo na yujyate |
atrākāśo nāmarūpanirvoḍhā paramaḥ pumān || 330 ||
nāmarūpāsparśarūpārthāntaratvopadeśataḥ |
tannāmarūpakartṛtvaṃ baddhamuktobhayātmataḥ || 331 ||
arthāntaratvaṃ vaktyasya baddhastadbhāgupādhitaḥ |
tannirvoḍhā na mukto'pi jagadvyāpāradūragaḥ || 332 ||
anena jīvenetyādivākyaistu paramātmanaḥ |
nirvoḍhṛtvaṃ tayornāmarūpayoḥ pratipāditam || 333 ||
dhūtvetyatrābhisaṃbhāvyatayaiva prakṛtaḥ paraḥ |
tasmādatrākāśaśabdavācyo nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 334 ||
|| suṣuptyutkrāntyorbhedena || 
aikyopadeśādbhedasya neheti ca niṣedhataḥ |
jīvādarthāntaro brahma neti vādo nirasyate || 335 ||
suṣuptyutkrāntikāleṣu prājñenetyādivākyayoḥ |
uktajīvapariṣvaṅgārohayoḥ pratyagātmataḥ || 336 ||
parasya bhedenokteśca tadanyo'sti paraḥ pumān |
svenaiva pariraṃbhādissvapato hi na saṃbhavet || 337 ||
|| patyādiśabdebhyaḥ || 
atra sarvasyādhipatiriti vākye parātmani |
prājñe śrutebhyaḥ patyādiśabdebhyaḥ pratyagātmataḥ || 338 ||
vilakṣaṇo'yamaikyoktistathā bhedaniṣedhavāk |
sarvasya brahmakāryatvaprayukteti samarthitam || 339 ||
|| ityarthāntaratvādivyapadeśādhikaraṇam ||
|| iti prathamādhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| atha caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| ānumānikādhikaraṇam || 
ānumānikamapyekeṣāmiti cenna śarīrarūpakavinyastagṛhīterdarśayati ca || 
pūrvatra spaṣṭajīvādiliṅgavākyaṃ vicāritam |
atrātyantaspaṣṭajīvaliṅgavākyaṃ vicāryate || 340 ||
atyantaspaṣṭajīvādiliṅgavākyavicāraṇāt |
lakṣaṇasya brahmabhinne'tivyāptirvāryate'tra ca || 341 ||
pūrvaṃ jīvātparaḥ kaścit neti vādo nirākṛtaḥ |
atra prakārāntareṇa pravṛttassa nivāryate || 342 ||
mahataḥ paramavyaktamiti mantre kaṭhaśruteḥ |
avyaktaśabdenoktaṃ tatpradhānamapi kāraṇam || 343 ||
tattantrapratyabhijñānātpuruṣātpañcaviṃśakāt |
puruṣānna paraṃ kiñcidityanyasya ca vāraṇāt || 344 ||
iti cenna rathatvena śarīraṃ yaddhi rūpitam |
tadgrāhyavyaktaśabdo'taḥ nāśabdaṃ tanna kāraṇam || 345 ||
ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhītyādipūrvoktamantrataḥ |
upāsanopāyabhūtā ātmadehādayaḥ kramāt || 346 ||
rūpitā ye rathitvādyairvaśīkāryatva eṣvatha |
parāḥ proktā indriyebhyaḥ parā ityādimantrataḥ || 347 ||
tatrendriyādayaste ca svasvaśabdena bhāṣitāḥ |
śarīraṃ tu rathatvena rūpitaṃ na svaśabdataḥ || 348 ||
pariśeṣātpūrvamuktavākyasthāvyaktaśabdataḥ |
tadeva gṛhyate'to'tra naiva tantrābhimarśanam || 349 ||
na pañcaviṃśakaḥ proktaḥ puruṣaḥ para ityataḥ |
vaśīkārye parāṃ kāṣṭhāṃ gataḥ prāpyaḥ paraḥ pumān || 350 ||
imamarthaṃ vākyaśeṣaḥ pradarśayati ca sphuṭam |
yacchedvāṅmanasī prājñastadyacchejjñāna ityapi || 351 ||
sūkṣmaṃ tu tadarhatvāt || 
śarīrāvasthamavyaktameva kāryārhamityataḥ |
śarīrameva tenehā'vyaktaśabdena gṛhyate || 352 ||
|| tadadhīnatvādarthavat || 
paramātmaśarīratvāttadadhīnatayā'rthavat |
bhūtasūkṣmamiti hyatrāvyaktaśabdena gṛhyate || 353 ||
|| jñeyatvāvacanācca || 
yadi tantraprakriyā syāt vyaktāvyakteti tadgiraḥ |
vaktavyā jñeyatā'syāpi noktā sā'to na sā matā || 354 ||
vadatīti cenna prājño hi prakaraṇāt || 
aśabdamiti vākyenā'vyaktasya jñeyatocyate |
naivaṃ prājñasya tatroktestasyaiva prakṛtatvataḥ || 355 ||
|| trayāṇāmeva caivamupanyāsaḥ praśnaśca || 
atra prakaraṇe yeyamityārabhyāsamāptitaḥ |
upāsyopāsanopāsitṝṇāṃ jñeyatayā bahuḥ || 356 ||
upanyāsastathā praśnastrayāṇāmeva dṛśyate |
tasmādavyaktasya neha grahaṇaṃ tāntrikasya hi || 357 ||
|| mahadvacca || 
buddherātmā mahānityatrātmaśabdāt yathā mahān |
na gṛhyate tathā'vyaktaśabdenāpi na tāntrikam || 358 ||
|| camasādhikaraṇam || 
|| camasavadaviśeṣāt || 
avyaktākhyapradhānasya svatantratvaṃ nirākṛtam |
ajāmantreṇa tatsiddhirityāśaṃkya nirasyate || 359 ||
śvetāśvataravede'jāmekāmityādi paṭhyate |
tatrājā tantrasiddhā syādakāryatvaśrutestathā || 360 ||
svātantryeṇa prajānāṃ ca sraṣṭṛtvaśravaṇādapi |
maivamabrahmātmikājā kāraṇatvena nocyate || 361 ||
janmāyogaśrutereva niyamo nahi tadgrahe |
yathārvāgbila ityādau camasākhye ca sādhane || 362 ||
śrutepi vākyaśeṣeṇa śirasaścamasatvadhīḥ |
tathātra tantrasiddhājāgrahaṇe na niyāmakam || 363 ||
sṛṣṭerhetutvamevoktaṃ na svātantryamihocyate |
|| jyotirupakramā tu tathā hyadhīyata eke || 
brahmakāraṇikaivaiṣā'jā tathā taittirīyakāḥ || 364 ||
adhīyate'ṇoraṇīyānityārabhya parātmataḥ |
sarvotpattyā tadātmatvavādimantragaṇasthale || 365 ||
ajāmekāmiti proktājā parātmātmikaiva sā |
|| kalpanopadeśācca madhvādivadavirodhaḥ || 
asmānmāyīti vākyena sṛṣṭeratropadeśataḥ || 366 ||
ajātvabrahmakāryatvadharmayorna parasparam |
virodho, layakāle tu nāmarūpe vihāya tat || 367 ||
brahmadehatayā tiṣṭhatyacidvastvajatā tataḥ |
bhajamānā sṛṣṭikāle te ajā brahmahetukā || 368 ||
ādityasya yathā sṛṣṭau madhutvaṃ kāryatā tathā |
tasyaiva layakāle tu sūkṣmasthitirakāryatā || 369 ||
|| iti camasādhikaraṇam ||
|| saṅkhyopasaṅgrahādhikaraṇam || 
|| na saṃkhyopasaṅgrahādapi nānābhāvādatirekācca || 
prāstamabrahmātmakatvamajāyā hetvabhāvataḥ |
yasminnityatra talliṅgamastītyetannirasyate || 370 ||
yasminpañceti mantro'sti bṛhadāraṇyakaśrutau |
eṣa mantrassāṃkhyatantratattvabodhaka iṣyate || 371 ||
pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyākatantratattvapratītitaḥ |
mokṣādhikārādetāni tāntrikāṇīti niścayaḥ || 372 ||
maivaṃ, yasminpañca pañcajanā ityādivākyataḥ |
pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyopasaṃgrahādapi tatpadāt || 373 ||
nātra tantrapratītissyādeṣāṃ tebhyaḥ pṛthaktvataḥ |
yasminniti padaproktabrahmāśrayatayā sadā || 374 ||
teṣāṃ brahmātmakatvaṃ hi jñāyate cātirekataḥ |
yacchabdavācyaścākāśaścādhiko pañcaviṃśateḥ || 375 ||
saṃkhyāsaṃgraha evātra nāstīti jñāpyate'pinā |
atra pañcajanābhikhyāḥ pañca santīti kathyate || 376 ||
|| prāṇādayo vākyaśeṣāt || 
pañca prāṇādayaḥ pañcajanā brahmāśrayā iti |
prāṇasya prāṇamityādivākyaśeṣātpratīyate || 377 ||
jyotiṣaikeṣāmasatyanne || 
asatyannepi kāṇvānāṃ pāṭhe pañcajanā ime |
indriyāṇīti budhyeta jyotiśśabdātpuroditāt || 378 ||
prakāśakānāṃ brahmoktvā prakāśakamanantaram |
pañcapañcetyuktitassyustādṛśyevendriyāṇi hi || 379 ||
ato yasminpañca pañcetyādinā paramātmani |
akṣabhūtasthitiprokternaiva kathyeta tāntrikam || 380 ||
|| iti saṃkhyopasaṃgrahādhikaraṇam ||
|| kāraṇatvādhikaraṇam || 
|| kāraṇatvena cākāśādiṣu yathāvyapadiṣṭokteḥ || 
abrahmātmapradhānasya kāraṇatvamavaidikam |
ityuktaṃ tatra vedānāmaikakaṇṭhyoktirasyate || 381 ||
sadevetyasadeveti kvacidavyākṛtaṃ tathā |
ucyate kāraṇaṃ vastu vākyaiḥ kāraṇabodhibhiḥ || 382 ||
brahmaṇaḥ kāraṇatve tu sattvāsattvādibādhataḥ |

|| bhoktrāpattyadhikaraṇam ||

|| ārambhaṇādhikaraṇam || 
||tadananyatvamārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ ||

sattvāccāparasya ||

paṭavacca ||

utpādakatvāpattiḥ syātkādācitkaṃ bhavenna tat || 166 ||
tataśca karmaṇāṇūnāṃ saṃyogābhāva āgataḥ |
vipākāpekṣayā nāsya sadotpādakateti cet || 167 ||
nānāprakārāddaṣṭānāmekadaivaikarūpataḥ |
na vipākassaṃbhaveddhi tatheśasyānumānataḥ || 168 ||
asiddherīśasaṅkalpāt niyamo'tra na saṃbhavet |
ato jagatkāraṇatvaṃ paramāṇorasaṅgatam || 169 ||
samavāyābhyupagamācca sāmyādanavasthiteḥ || 
samāvāyābhyupagamāccedaṃ matamasaṅgatam|
dravyāprathaksiddhihetutayā jātyādikasya ca || 170 ||
iṣyate samavāyo'yaṃ tathābhāvepyamuṣya ca |
sāmyācca hetvapekṣāyāḥ anavasthā prasajyate || 171 ||
svābhāvikastathābhāvassambandhasyeti cettadā |
eṣa svabhāvo jātyādereva yuktaḥ kimanyataḥ || 172 ||
nityameva ca bhāvāt || 
sambandhasyāsya nityatve sambandhyakhilavastunaḥ |
nityameva ca bhāvāddhi matametadasaṅgatam || 173 ||
rūpādimattvācca viparyayo darśanāt || 
pārthivādyaṇusaṅghānāmeṣāṃ rūpādimattvataḥ |
syātsvābhimatanitya tvā'naṃśitvādiviparyayaḥ || 174||
ghaṭādikeṣu rūpādimatsvevaṃ darśanānnahi |
ddaṣṭānusārātkalpyo'rthaḥ svabhīṣṭe paryavasyati || 175 ||
ubhayathā ca doṣāt || 
uktānityatvādidoṣabhayāt rūpādiśūnyatā |
aṇusaṅghe svīkṛtā cetkāryaṃ syādrūpavarjitam || 176 ||
svahetuguṇapūrvatvāt guṇānāṃ kāryavartinām |
etaddoṣabhayātteṣu rūpavattvaṃ yadīṣyate || 177 |
tarhyanityatvādidoṣaḥ pūrvoktaḥ prasajettataḥ |
doṣādubhayathā caivamasamañjasameva tat || 178 ||
aparigrahāccātyantamanapekṣā || 
śrutinyāyaviruddhepi kapilasya mate kvacit |
satkāryavādamukhye'sti vaidikānāṃ parigrahaḥ || 179 ||
kenāpyaṃśena kāṇādapakṣasyāsyāparigrahāt |
anapekṣaiva kāryā'trātyantaṃ mokṣaphalepsubhiḥ || 180 ||
|| iti mahaddīrghādhikaraṇam ||
samudāyādhikaraṇam || 
samudāya ubhayahetuke'pi tadaprāptiḥ || 
aṇukāraṇatāvādipakṣe pūrvaṃ nirākṛte |
tatsāmyopāsthitaḥ pakṣaḥ saugatānāṃ nirasyate || 181 ||
caturvidhāssaugatāsteṣvādyā vaibhāṣikākhyakāḥ |
dvitīyāssautrāntikākhyā eṣāṃ darśanamīddaśam || 182 ||
gandhādisaṅghairaṇubhiḥ pārthivādyaiścaturvidhaiḥ |
utpādyante pṛthivyādisaṅdhātāstaiśca bhautikāḥ || 183 ||
utpādyante ca saṅghātāḥ bāhyārthā eta eva hi |
āntaro jñānasantānarūpo dehāntarāsthitaḥ || 184 ||
ahaṃpratītiviṣayaḥ ātmāstyartheṣvamīṣvapi |
kṣaṇikeṣu sthiratvādibuddhyākhyāvidyayā'khilam || 185 ||
upapadyata evātaḥ jagadutpattirapyatha |
yujyate vyavahāraścetyetanmatamasaṅgatam || 186 ||
aṇuhetukapṛthvyādisaṅghe pṛthvyādihetuke |
dehendriyādisaṅghe ca saṅghotpattirna yujyate || 187 ||
kṣaṇikatvābhyupagamāt aṇupṛthvyādikasya ca |
kadā'ṇavaḥ kṣaṇadhvastāḥ vyāpriyante ca saṃhatau || 188 ||
bhūtāni ca kadā vā te saṃhanyante'tha tāni ca |
kadā te jñānaviṣayāḥ yānti vā vyavahāryatām || 189 ||
sarve kṣaṇaviśiṣṭāścet padārthā, nahi yujyate |
jagadutpattirapi ca vyavahāraśca laukikaḥ || 190 ||
itaretarapratyayatvādupapannamiti cenna saṅghātabhāvānimittatvāt || 
avidyā viparītā dhīḥ tayā saṃskāraśabditāḥ |
rāgadveṣādayaścaivamavidyādiparamparā || 191 ||
anyonyamūlā'nādiśca cakravatparivartate |
bhūtabhautikasaṅghātaṃ vinaitannopapadyate || 192 ||
atassaṅghātabhāvādyamupapannamiheti cet |
naivametadavidyādeḥ saṅghātatvādikaṃ prati || 193 ||
animittatvato'vidyā sthiratābuddhirasthire |
kṣaṇikasya hi nārthasya bhajet saṃhatihetutām || 194 ||
śuktirūpyatvabuddhirhi na śuktyādyarthasaṃhateḥ |
hetussyāt kiñca yasya syāt kṣaṇike sthiratāmatiḥ || 195 ||
sa tadaiva dhvasta iti kasya rāgādikaṃ bhavet |
uttaro pāde ca pūrvanirodhāt || 
parakṣaṇotpattikāle pūrvakṣaṇavināśataḥ || 196 ||
parakṣaṇaṃ pratyetasya hetutvānupapattitaḥ |
na syādviśvotpattirasmin kṣaṇikatvamate dhruvam || 197 ||
abhāvasyaiva hetutve sarvaṃ sarvatra sarvadā |
utpadyetā'tha pūrvatra vartino'syaiva hetutā || 198 ||
kaścit ghaṭakṣaṇo hetuḥ syānnānottarabhāvinām |
asati pratijñoparodho yaugapadyamanyathā || 
asatyapi ca hetau tat kāryamutpadyate yadi || 199 ||
jñānotpattāvadhipatisahakāryādikasya ca |
kāraṇatvapratijñāyāḥ virodhaścāpi saṃbhavet || 200 ||
pratijñā'nuparodhāya tiṣṭhatyekaghaṭakṣaṇe |
ghaṭakṣaṇāntarotpattiḥ iṣyate bhavatā yadi || 201 ||
yaugapadyenopalabdhiḥ kāryakāraṇayorbhavet |
pratisaṃkhyā'pratisaṃkhyānirodhāprāptiravicchedāt || 
vināśaśca dvidhā proktaḥ sthūlasūkṣmaprabhedataḥ || 202 ||
ādyo'yamupalabdhyarhaḥ ghaṭādau daṇḍattāḍite |
antyaḥ pratikṣaṇodbhāvī tadanarho niranvayau || 203 ||
na syātāṃ tau vināśādidharmabhāgvastunassadā |
avicchedāt ghaṭādau cāvasthāntarasamanvite || 204 ||
niranvayavināśasyādarśanādevame tu |
dīpanirvāṇe'pi sūkṣmāvasthāsaṃprāptiriṣyate || 205 ||
ubhayathā ca doṣāt || 
jātasya tucchatāpattau tucchāccotpattivarṇane |
doṣāpatterubhayathā te na syātāṃ hi sarvathā ||206 ||
tucchādutpattivāde hi kāryaṃ tucchātmakaṃ bhavet |
yasmādutpadyate yacca tat tadātmakameva hi || 207 ||
mṛtsuvarṇādikāryaṃ hi dṛśyate tanmayaṃ yathā |
jātasya tucchatāpattau jaguttacechātmakaṃ bhavet || 208 ||
tacca tucchātmakaṃ nāṅgīkriyate na pramīyate |
tasmādubhayathā doṣādidaṃ matamasaṅgatam || 209 ||
ākāśe cāviśeṣāt || 
tucchasvarūpatā''kāśe na saṃbhavati tatra ca |
abādhitapratītiryat pṛthibyādāvivāsti naḥ || 210 ||
patatyatra śyena iti tadādhāratvato'niśam |
anusmṛteśca || 
pratyabhijñānatopyevamarthakṣaṇikatā nahi ||
tadevedamiti jñānamatītādyavagāhanāt |
kāladvayaviśiṣṭaṃ ca vastvekaṃ sūcayatyataḥ || 212 ||
asthiratve padārthasya pratyabhijñā na saṅgatā |
samānaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ bāhyārthāstitvavādinoḥ || 213 ||
nāsato'dṛṣṭatvāt || 
jñānotpattikṣaṇe'rthasyānavasthānānna kasyacit |
arthasya dhīviṣayatā syāditi prāk yadīritam || 214 ||
tatrānumeyārthavādī prāha sautrāntikaḥ paraḥ |
na jñānaviṣayatve'tra vastusattaiva kāraṇam || 215 ||
vastuno dhīviṣayatā yatastaddhetutātmikā |
vijñāne nīlapītādivicitrākāradarśanāt || 216 ||
pūrvaṃ samarpya svākāraṃ vijñāne tadanantaram |
svayaṃ vinaṣṭo nīlādirarthaḥ pusā'numīyate || 217 ||
yato'rthakṛtamevaitat jñānavaicitryamityasat |
jñāne nīlādirākāraḥ labhyate yaḥ sa nārhati || 218 ||
ākāro bhavituṃ naṣṭasyārthasyaivamapi kvacit |
naṣṭe dharmiṇyadṛṣṭatvāt dharmasyānyatra saṅgateḥ || 219 ||
japāpuṣpe vinaṣṭe hi lauhityaṃ sphaṭike kutaḥ |
dhīvaicitryamato'rthasya kṣaṇikatve na saṃbhavet || 220 ||
udāsīnānāmapi caivaṃ siddhiḥ || 
kṣaṇikatve padārthānāṃ niṣprayatnanṛṇāmapi |
sarvārthasiddhissyādeva prayatnādyanutiṣṭhataḥ || 221 ||
tadānīmeva naṣṭatvāt tatsaṃskārādikasya ca |
sthirasyaikasyānuvartamānasyāsaṃmatatvataḥ || 222 ||
bhavedahaitukī siddhiḥ aprayatnavatāmapi |
ubhayorapyarya doṣaḥ samo'to darśanaṃ na sat || 223 ||
|| iti samudāyādhikaraṇam ||
upalabdhyadhikaraṇam || 
nā'bhāva upalabdheḥ || 
caturvidheṣu bauddheṣu prāṅnirastaṃ dvayormatam |
yogācārasyātra mataṃ tṛtīyasya nirasyate || 224 ||
yaddhīvicitratvamarthavaicitryakṛtamucyate |
tannārthavanmatereva vicitratvācca sākṛteḥ || 225 ||
nirākāradhiyāṃ kutrāpyaprakāśo hi saṃmataḥ |
tataḥ prakāśamānāyāḥ dhiyassākāratā bhavet || 226 ||
jñāyamāno ya ākāraḥ sa jñānasyeti niścayaḥ |
bahirvadavabhāso'pi tasya vibhramamūlakaḥ || 227 ||
sahopalambhaniyamādapi jñānārthayostathā |
jñānādavyatirikto'rthaḥ kiñca bāhyārthavādibhiḥ || 228 ||
jñānepyarthasarūpaṃ ca rūpamāstheyamanyathā |
jñānasya tattadarthaṃ pratyasādhāraṇatā katham || 229 ||
tāvanmātreṇaiva sarvavyavahāropapattitaḥ |
vijñānetarabāhyārthakalpanaṃ naiva yuktimat || 230 ||
iti yogācāramataṃ nyāye'muṣminnirasyate |
vijñānavyatiriktārthābhāvo vaktuṃ na śakyate || 231 ||
jānāmyahaṃ ghaṭamiti nirābādhopalabdhitaḥ |
jñātṛjñānārtheṣu tulyaṃ sphuratsvatra pṛthak triṣu || 232 ||
jñānasyaikasya satyatvamasatyatvaṃ tadanyayoḥ |
vadato'sya mataṃ nūnaṃ hāsyaṃ niryuktakatvataḥ || 233 ||
sahopalaṃbhaniyamāt abheda iti tadvacaḥ |
viruddhamarthabhedasya sāhityaṃ sādhakaṃ yataḥ || 234 ||
tadarthavyavahārānuguṇyāpādakataiva hi |
tadjñānasyākāra iti ghaṭādirna tathā bhavet || 235 ||
jñānasya yasya yenāsti sambandho viṣayeṇa tat |
jñānaṃ tadviṣayāsādhāraṇamīdṛgvyavasthayā || 236 ||
ghaṭādyasādhāraṇatvaṃ tadjñānasya hi kīrtyate |
vaidharmyācca na svanpādivat || 
svānpajñānasyeva bāhyaghaṭādyarthadhiyāmapi || 237 ||
arthaśūnyatvasiddhyā ca bāhyārthā neti cenna tat |
jāgraddaśādhiyāṃ svanpajñānavaidharmyato bhṛśam || 238 ||
arthaśūnyatvavādo na yujyate, svanpagā dhiyaḥ |
nidrāduṣṭandriyājjanyā bādhitāśca bhavanti hi || 239 ||
jāgraddaśādhiyaścaitadviparītā na tatsamāḥ |
vastutassvānpike jñānepyarthasatyatvamiṣyate || 240 ||
jñānānāmarthaśūnyatve sādhyopyartho na siddhyati |
nirālambānumānasyāpyarthaśūnyatvatastava || 241 ||
tasyārthavattve jñānatvamanekāntaṃ bhavet khalu |
na bhāvo'nupalabdheḥ || 
bhāvo'rthaśūnyajñānasya na saṃbhavati ca kvacit || 242 ||
akartṛkākarmakasya jñānasyānupalabdhitaḥ ||
ato yogācāramataṃ sarvamapyasamañjasam || 243 ||
|| ityupalabdhyadhikaraṇam ||
sarvathānupapattyadhikaraṇam ||
sarvathānupapatteśca || 
bauddhaikadeśināṃ pakṣāḥ trividhānāṃ nirākṛtāḥ |
atha mādhyamikassarvaśūnyavādī nirasyate || 244 ||
utparttidurnirūpaiva bhāvato'bhāvatastathā |
ghaṭādiścotpadyamānaḥ mṛdāderupamarditāt || 245 ||
utpadyate copamardaḥ nāśastasyā''kṛtessa ca |
abhāva eva tasmānna bhāvādutpattiriṣyate || 246 ||
nāntyaḥ pakṣopyabhāvācca bhāvotpatterasaṃbhavāt |
ittha ca durnirūpatvādutpattestadanantarāḥ || 247 ||
vikārāśca na santyevā'tastattvaṃ śūnyamucyate |
mataṃ mādhyamikasyedaṃ sarvathā nopapadyate || 248 ||
sarvaṃ saditi vā sarvamasadityeva vānyathā |
śūnyavādī pratijñāya yateta sveṣṭasādhane || 249 ||
sarvathānupapatteśca tadiṣṭaṃ tu na saṃbhavet |
vastuno vidyamānasya sadamacchabdataddhiyoḥ || 250 ||
avasthāgocaratvaṃ hi proktaṃ tasmānna tucchatā |
kutaścidapi siddhyettadabhipretā hi sarvathā || 251 ||
iṣyate yadi nāstitvaṃ akhilaṃ śūnyamityataḥ |
tarhi rūpāntareṇaivāstitvaṃ proktaṃ bhavet, yataḥ || 252 ||
rūpāntarāpattireva nāstiśabdārtha ucyate |
tato mṛtpiṇḍopamardaḥ ghaṭatvotpattirūpakaḥ || 253 ||
mṛddravyaṃ piṇḍatāvasthāyuktamutpādakaṃ, tathā |
tadeva ghaṭatāvasthāyuktamutpādyamiṣyate || 254 ||
tasmādabhāvādbhāvasya notpattiḥ kvacidiṣyate |
tasmānmādhyamikasyedaṃ mataṃ sarvamasaṅgatam || 255 ||
|| iti sarvathānupapattyadhikaraṇam ||
|| ekasminnasaṃbhavādhikaraṇam || 
naikasminnasaṃbhavāt || 
nirasyaivaṃ saugatoktimārhatasya mataṃ tviha |
aṇukāraṇatāvādasāmyopasthitamasyate ||256 ||
jagat jīvādibhedena ṣaḍdravyātmakamīśvaraḥ |
na kaścidastatra jīvaḥ dehatulyapramāṇavān ||257 ||
sadevetyādiniyamaśūnyaṃ vastvakhilaṃ ca tat |
sarvaṃ ca sadasadrūpaṃ nityānityātmakaṃ tathā || 258 ||
bhinnābhinnātmakamapītyevaṃ jainamataṃ na sat |
sattvāsattvādidharmāṇāṃ tejastimiravanmithaḥ || 259 ||
viruddhānāmekadaivāsaṃbhavādekavastuni |
naitanmataṃ saṅgataṃ syāt, aśvatvamahiṣatvayoḥ ||260 ||
kathaṃ yugapadekasmin samāveśasya saṃbhavaḥ |
īśānādhiṣṭhitāṇośca kāraṇatve ya īritāḥ || 261 ||
doṣāste tadavasthāścetyayuktaṃ jainajalpanam |
evaṃ cātmā'kātsnaryam || 
jīvasyaivaṃ vapustulyamānābhyupagame sati || 262 ||
hastidehasthitasyāsya maśakādipraveśane |
alpadeśavyāpitayā bhavedaparipūrṇatā || 263 ||
bṛhaddehapraveśe'pi kṛtsnavyāptirna tatra ca |
na ca paryāyādapyavirodho vikārādibhyaḥ || 
na ca saṅkocādirūpāvasthābhyupanamena ca || 264 ||
parihartuṃ virodho'sya śakyate ghaṭatulyatā |
vikāritvānityatādidoṣāspadatayā bhavet || 265 ||
antyāvasthiteścobhayanityatvādaviśeṣaḥ|| 
ātmano mokṣakāle yatparimāṇaṃ tadeva ca |
svābhāvikaṃ tataścātmasvarūpavadamuṣya ca || 266 ||
mokṣakālikamānasya nityatvātpūrvamapyadaḥ |
mānameveti vaktavyatayā tasyātmavastunaḥ || 267 ||
pūrvaṃ vapustulyamānavado'tīvāsamañjasaḥ |
tasmānmitho viruddhatvāt matametadasaṅgatam || 268 ||
ityekasminnasaṃbhavādhikaraṇam ||
paśupatyadhikaraṇam || 
patyurasāmañjasyāt || 
kāpilādimateṣvastu vedabāhyeṣu mādaraḥ |
sarvajñarudrasaṃprokte mate pāśupate bhṛśam ||
ādaraḥ kārya eveti vādo'tra vinivāryate |
īśassiddho'numānena sa aupaniṣado na ca || 270 ||
nimittopādānayośca bhedaḥ paśupatisthā |
nimittaheturviścopādānamavyākṛtaṃ bhavet || 271 ||
jagatpaśuṣatissarvaṃ kulālādirghaṭādivat |
sṛjatyevaṃ muktihetuḥ mudrikāṣāṭkadhāraṇam ||272 ||
surākumbhe devapūjā cātmadhyānaṃ bhage tathā |
iti vedaviruddhārthān śaivā āhuścaturvidhāḥ || 273 ||
idaṃ mataṃ paśupateḥ vyāghātācca parasparam |
śruteścaivaṃ viruddhatvāt asāmañjasyato bhṛśam ||274 ||
niśreyasārthibhirnaivā''daraṇīyaṃ vivekibhiḥ |
vedā jagannimittaṃ copādānaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ || 275 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ vadantasya brahmaṇo vadenātmikā |
upāstirmuktidā ceti tatra tatra śrutā api || 276 ||
śivādiśabdā nārāyaṇānuvākānusārataḥ |
nārāyaṇaparā eva śrutaṃ tatra tathaiva hi || 277 ||
ayaṃ cārthaḥ pūrvameva suspaṣṭaṃ pratipāditaḥ |
ato vedaviruddhatvāt matametadaṅgatam || 278 ||
adhiṣṭhānānupapatteśca || 
nimittamātreśvarasya kalpanādanumānataḥ |
dṛṣṭānusārātkartavyamadhiṣṭhānaṃ kulālavat || 279 ||
na yujyate paśupateḥ pradhānādhiṣṭhitiḥ kvacit |
adehatvātsadehānāṃ hyevādhiṣṭhitiriṣyate || 280 ||
na śarīritvamasya syāttasya sāvayavasya ca |
nityatve'nityatāyāṃ ca prāk doṣāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 281 ||
karaṇavaccenna bhogādibhyaḥ || 
aśarīrasya jīvasya karaṇādiparigrahaḥ |
yathopapadyate tadvadīśvarasyāpyadehinaḥ || 282 ||
prakṛtyadhiṣṭhānamiti cenna puṇyādikarmaṇām |
phalabhogāya hi vapurādyadhiṣṭhānamātmanaḥ || 283 ||
tadvadasyāpi sarvaṃ syātphalabhogādyapīśituḥ |
tasmātpradhānādhiṣṭhānasaṃbhavo neśvarasya hi || 284 ||
antavattvamasarvaṃjñatā vā || 
puṇyāpuṇyātmakādṛṣṭavattve paśupatestadā |
antavattvaṃ jīvavacca sṛṣṭisaṃhāryatā tathā || 285 ||
asarvajñatvaṃ ca bhavet tasmātsarvaprakārataḥ |
idaṃ nādaraṇīyaṃ syāt mataṃ muktiphalepsubhiḥ || 286 ||
|| iti paśupatyadhikaraṇam ||
utpattyasaṃbhavādhikaraṇam ||
utpattyasaṃbhavāt || 
sāṅravyāditantrasāmānyātpāñcarātrāgame'pi ca |
sākṣādbhagavatā prokte mokṣasādhanabodhini || 287 ||
aprāmāṇyāśaṅkiparapakṣo'tra kṣipyate nayaiḥ |
asaṃbhavantyā jīvānāmutpatterabhidhānataḥ || 288 ||
na pramāṇaṃ pāñcarātrāgamastantre'tra kathyate |
saṅkarṣaṇo nāma jīvaḥ vāsudevātprajāyate || 289 ||
saṅkarṣaṇācca pradyamnasaṃjñamutpadyate manaḥ |
ahaṅkāro'niruddhākhyaḥ tasmājjāyata ityapi || 290 ||
atra śrutiviruddhā hi jīvotpattiḥ pritīyate |
śrutau " na jāyata " iti tadanāditvamucyate || 291 ||
na ca kartuḥ karaṇam || 
saṅkarṣaṇānmano jātamiti kartuśca jīvataḥ |
manasaḥ karaṇasyaivamutpattiśca na saṃbhavet ||
" etasmājjāyate prāṇaḥ manassarvendriyāṇi ca " |
ityutpattiśruterbrahmasakāśānmanaso'pi ca || 293 ||
vijñānādibhāve vā tadapratiṣedhaḥ || 
evaṃ sūtradvayenaitadāgamasyāpramāṇatām |

vipratiṣedhācca ||

upādānādvipāropadeśācca ||
