upadiṣṭā tataścāyameva kartā bhavetkhalu || 421 ||
vyapadeśācca kriyāyāṃ nacennirdeśaviparyayaḥ || 
vijñānaṃ tanute yajñaṃ karmāṇi tanute'pi ca|
iti sarvakriyāsvasya kartṛtvavyapadeśataḥ || 422 ||
kartā'yaṃ , nanu vijñānaśabdānnātmocyate'pitu |
antaḥ karaṇarūpoktā buddhireveti cenna tat || 423 ||
tathā satyatra nirdeśaviparyāso bhavetkhalu |
vijñāneneti karaṇavibhaktiḥ paṭhitā bhavet || 424 ||
tato vijñānaśabdena jīvātmaivocyate śrutau |
upalabdhivadaniyamaḥ || 
upalabdheraniyamaḥ vibhutve cātmanaḥ purā || 425 ||
ya uktastadvadatrāpi hyakartṛtve'sya cātmanaḥ |
prakṛteścāpi kartṛtve bhoktṛtvāniyamo bhavet || 426 ||
prakṛtessarvapuruṣasāmānyādakhilaḥ kriyāḥ |
bhogāya syuḥ sarvapuṃsāṃ naiva vā yasya kasyacit || 427 ||
antaḥkaraṇabheda'pi hyātmanāṃ sarvagatvataḥ |
sādhāraṇyaṃ cānivāryaṃ vyavasthā'to na siddhyati || 428 ||
tataścāsyaiva kartṛtvaṃ , prakṛternahi sarvathā |
śaktiviparyayāt || 
prakṛtessati kartṛtve tadanyasya tu sarvāthā || 429 ||
bhoktṛtvāyogatastasyā eva syāt bhoktṛtā tataḥ |
ātmano bhoktṛtāśaktirna syātsā prakṛteriti || 430 ||
mānaṃ nātmasthitau tasmātkartā bhoktā ca cetanaḥ |
samādhyabhāvācca || 
kartṛtve prakṛtestasyāḥ samādhau mokṣasādhane || 431 ||
sā syātkartrī samādhiśca prakṛtyanyosmyahantviti |
prakṛtiḥ prakṛteranyāsmīti dhyātumalaṃ na hi || 432 ||
yathā ca takṣebhayadhā || 
vāgādīndriyasampannopyātmecchati yadā tadā |
karoti necchati yadā na karoti tadā yathā || 433 ||
takṣā vāsyādiyuktopi svecchayaiva karoti ca |
na karoti ca , kartatve prakṛteracitastathā || 434 ||
vāñcchādihetvabhāvāddhi sarvadā kartṛtā bhavet |
tasmāt kartṛtvabhoktṛtve jīvasyaiveti susthitam || 435 ||
|| iti kartradhikaraṇam ||
parāyattādhikaraṇam || 
parāttu tacchateḥ || 
pūrvaṃ jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ yat vyavasthāpitaṃ nayaiḥ |
tat parātmādhīnamiti sthāpyate'dhikṛtāviha || 436 ||
svāyattameva kartṛtvaṃ na parāyattamātmanaḥ |
parādhīnaṃ yadi syāttat vidhyādervyarthatā''patet || 437 ||
yaḥ svabuddhyā pravṛttyādaśaktaḥ preryassa eva hi |
tasmājjīvasya kartṛtvaṃ svātantryeṇeti cenna tat || 438 ||
kartṛtvamasya jīvasya parātmayattameva hi |
antaḥ praviṣṭaśśāstā , ya ātmanī tyādivākyataḥ || 439 ||
sarvasya cāha mityādigītāvacanatastathā |
ato jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ parātmādhinamiṣyate || 440 ||
kṛtaprayatrāpekṣastu vihitapratiṣiddhāvaiyarthyādibhyaḥ || 
sarvakriyāsu jīvena prathamaṃ svecchayā kṛtam |
yatraṃ parātmāpekṣyātha tatrānumatidānataḥ || 441 ||
pravartayatyayaṃ cārthaḥ vidhyavaiyarthyatastathā |
nigrahānugrabhyāṃ ca jñāyate'taḥ parātmanā || 442 ||
pravartito'pi jīvātmā yogyo vidhiniṣedhayoḥ |
tasmājjīvasya kartṛtbaṃ parāyattamiti sthitam || 443 ||
audāsīnyānumantṛtve prerakatvamitīśituḥ |
ākārāssanti tatrādyapravṛttau pratyagātmanaḥ || 444 ||
audāsīnyaṃ , dvitīyādipravṛttāvanumantṛtā |
tadabhāve pravṛtterapyātmano'saṃbhavāttathā || 445 ||
ya īśasyānukūlye vā prātikūlye'thavā pumān |
atyantavyavasāyyasmin viṣaye prerakatvavān || 446 ||
|| iti parāyattādhikaraṇam ||
|| aṃśādhikaraṇam || 
aṃśo nānāvyapadeśādanyathā cāpi dāśakatitavāditvamadhīyata eke || 
pūrvaṃ jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ parāyattamitītaritam |
tena jīvabrahmaṇośca rājatadbhṛtyayoriva || 447 ||
atyantabhedāvagamāt tadabhedārthakaśruteḥ |
āśrityāmukhyatāṃ prāpto vādo'tra vinivāryate || 448 ||
jīvaḥ parātmato'tyantabhinno jñājña viti śruteḥ |
abhedaśrutayo'mukhyāḥ viruddhārthāvabodhanāt || 449 ||
bhrāntaṃ brahmaiva vā jīvaḥ brahmātmatvopadeśataḥ |
ayamātmā brahma , tattvamasī tyādyabhidāśrutau || 450 ||
bhedaśrutistu pratyakṣasiddhabhedānuvādinī |
yadvā brahmopādhiyuktaṃ jīva uktābhidāśruteḥ || 451 ||
asyopādherbhrāntiparikalpitatvaṃ na saṃbhavet |
bandhamekṣavyavasthāyāssarvathā'nupapattitaḥ || 452 ||
bhedaśrutistu gauṇyeva , neśāṃśo jīva iṣyate |
ekavastvekadeśatvaṃ hyaṃśatvaṃ nāsya tadbhavet || 453 ||
tasya brahmaikadeśatve taddoṣaudhaḥ pare bhavet |
na brahmakhaṇḍo jīvo'sya khaṇḍanāderasaṃbhavāt || 454 ||
iti cenna brahmaṇoṃśaḥ jīvo bhedopadeśataḥ |
abhedavyapadeśācca , parasyaike ca śākhinaḥ || 455 ||
brahma dāśā iti śrutyā dāśatvādyapyadhīyate |
etena sarvajīvātmavyāpitvenābhidocyate || 456 ||
viśiṣṭavastvekadeśo'pṛthaksiddhaviśeṣaṇam |
yat, tadeva dravyasaṃśaśabdavācyaṃ bhavediha || 457 ||
cetanācetanābhyāṃ ca viśiṣṭaṃ brahma sarvadā |
jīvasya brahmadehatvaṃ yasyātme tyādinā śrutam || 458 ||
śarīraṃ hyapṛthaksiddhadravyabhūtaviśeṣaṇam |
ato jīvasya pūrvoktamaṃśatvaṃ syātparaṃ prati || 459 ||
śarīravāciśabdānāṃ dehiparyantavācitā |
ākṛtinyāyasiddhā'taḥ śabdānāṃ jīvavācinām || 460 ||
brahmaparyantavācitvāt tattvasyādiṣu śrutam |
sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ hi mukhyaṃ syāllakṣaṇāṃ vinā || 461 ||
bhedaśrutistu sarvatra svarūpeṇa svabhāvataḥ |
jīveśayormitho bhedaṃ vaktyato'syāśca mukhyatā || 462 ||
tasmātsiddhāntināṃ sarvaśrutermukhyatvamakṣatam |
ato jīvaḥ parātyantabhinnaḥ syāditi vāk hatā || 463 ||
nityanirduṣṭasarvajñabrahmaṇo bhrāntatādaśā |
na kadāpi bhavettasmātpakṣo'yamapi vāritaḥ || 464 ||
parasya sukhaduḥkhāderāśrayatvaprasaṅgataḥ |
tṛtīyaścāpi pakṣo'yaṃ sudūraṃ vinivāritaḥ || 465 ||
tasmājjīvo brahmaṇoṃśaḥ iti pakṣaḥ samañjasaḥ |
mantravarṇāt || 
pādo'sya viśvābhūtānī tyādipuṃsūktamantrataḥ || 466 ||
brahmāṃśa eva jīvo'tra pādaśabdoṃ'śavācakaḥ |
api smaryate || 
mamaivāṃśo jīvaloke jīvabhūtassanātanaḥ || 467 ||
iti jīvasya gītāyāṃ smaryate hi paraṃśatā |
prakāśādivattu naivaṃ paraḥ || 
jīvaḥ prakāśādiriva paramātmāṃśa ucyate || 468 ||
yathā prakāśo maṇyāderaṃśaḥ syāttadviśeṣaṇam |
tathātmadehakatvavāttadviśiṣṭasya paratmanaḥ || 469 ||
jīvoṃśaḥ syādviśiṣṭaikadeśarūpaviśeṣaṇam |
viśiṣṭasyāṃśa eva syādevaṃ jātiguṇādayaḥ || 470 ||
viśeṣaṇatvaikarūpāḥ dravyāṃśā eva sarvadā |
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyākhyajīvātmeśvarayordvayoḥ || 471 ||
svarūpasya svabhāvasyātyantabhedāt parasparam |
yatsvarūpo yatsvabhāvo jīvo naivaṃ paro bhavet || 472 ||
sarvajñassatyasaṅkalpo niravadyaḥ paraḥ pumānu |
smaranti ca || 
parāśarādayoṃśatvaṃ prabhāyā iva cātmanaḥ || 473 ||
ekadeśasthitasyāgnerjyotsne tyādau smaranti ca |
yatkiñcitsṛjyate yene tyādinā ca paraṃ prati || 474 ||
tanutvamasya jīvasya suspaṣṭaṃ kathayanti hi |
anujñāparihārau dehasaṃbandhāt jyotirādivat || 
dvijādisarvajīvānāṃ brahmāṃśatve same'pi ca || 475 ||
vedāpāṭhādyanujñātanniṣedhau yeṣukeṣucit |
śucyaśucyātmaviprādidehayogakṛtau , yathā || 476 ||
agnirāhniyate''gārāt śrotriyasya , śmaśānagaḥ |
sa parihniyate kiñca yathānnaṃ śrotriyasya hi || 477 ||
grāhyaṃ tadabhiśastādeḥ tyājyaṃ tadvadihocyate |
asantateścavyatikaraḥ || 
jīvānāṃ pratidehaṃ ca bhinnatvādaṇumānataḥ || 478 ||
mitho jīvavibhedācca bhogāvyatikaro bhavet |
brahmaiva baddhyate'jñānopādhibhyāmiti pakṣayoḥ || 479 ||
bhogavyavasthitirnaiva syādato'sādhu taddvayam |
ābhāsā eva ca|| 
sarvajñabrahmaṇo'jñānatattirodhānasādhakāḥ || 480 ||
ābhāsā hetavaḥ , syācca bādho nirdoṣatāśruteḥ|
avidyākalpitopādhibhedavāde'pi pūrvavat || 481 ||
satyopādhiyutabrahmajīvavāde'pyadṛṣṭataḥ |
upādhibhedahetostadvyavasthetyatra kathyate || 482 ||
adṛṣṭāniyamāt || 
upādhihetvadṛṣṭānāmāśrayasya parasya ca |
ekatvāttadadṛṣṭānāṃ niyamāsiddhitastathā || 483 ||
upādhibhiradṛṣṭaiśca svasambandhātparasya hi |
chedāsaṃbhavataścāpi na syāt bhogavyavasthitiḥ || 484 ||
abhisandhyādiṣvapi caivam || 
adṛṣṭahetubhūteṣu saṅkalpādiṣvapīśituḥ |
pūrvoktātkāraṇādeva tadvyavasthā na sebhavet || 485 ||
pradeśabhedāditi cennāntarbhāvāt || 
upādhiyogino brahmapradeśāttadayoginaḥ |
brahmapradeśā bhidyānte tasmādbhogavyavasthitiḥ || 486 ||
iti cettanna , gacchatsu sarvatropādhiṣu svayam |
antarbhāvātpradeśasya pūrvānupahitasya ca || 487 ||
tasmādbhogavyatikaraḥ durvāraḥ , sakalasya ca |
brahmadeśatvatassarvaduḥkhaṃ tasyaiva saṃbhavet || 488 ||
ataḥ parasmin cidacidviśiṣṭabrahmaṇi svayam |
viśeṣaṇatvenāṃśo'yaṃ jīva ityeva susthitam || 489 ||
|| ityaṃśādhikaraṇam ||
|| iti dvatīyādhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| dvitīyādhyāye caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| prāṇotpattyadhikaraṇam || 
tathā prāṇāḥ || 
prāguktaṃ brahmakāryatvaṃ khāderjīvātmanāmapi |
atrendriyādijīvopakaraṇānāṃ taducyate || 490 ||
yathā notpadyate jīvaḥ tathaivendriyasañcayaḥ |
prāksṛṣṭerindriyāṇāmapyavasthānapramāṇataḥ || 491 ||
ṛṣayo vāva te , ke te , prāṇā vāve ti ca śrutiḥ |
pralaye'pi sthitiṃ teṣāṃ bravītyutpattivādinī || 492 ||
jīvotpattiśrutiriva neyetyetanmataṃ na sat |
prāk sṛṣṭeḥ paramātmaikatvāvadhāraṇatastathā || 493 ||
etasmāñjāyate prāṇaḥ ityutpattiśrutesyathā |
tannityatvavaco'bhāvāccodbhavantīndriyāṇi tu || 494 ||
prāṇā vāve tyādinoktaḥ parātmā'syaiva saṃbhavet |
ṛṣiśabdenāvagataṃ sarvajñatvaṃ , nacā'citām || 495 ||
prāṇā ṛṣaya ityatra bahūktissaṃbhavetkatham |
bahutvāsaṃbhavādatretyāśaṅkaivaṃ nivāryate || 496 ||
gauṇyasaṃbhavāt tatprākśruteśca || 
bahūktiratra goṇī syāt bahṇarthāsaṃbhāttadā |
paramātmana eva prāk avasthānaśruterapi || 497 ||
tatpūrvakatvādvācaḥ || 
nāmadheyasya sarvasya paramātmānyavācinaḥ |
viyadādessṛṣṭipūrvakatvāt prāṇāḥ paraḥ pumān || 498 ||
prāksṛṣṭerviyadādīnāmabhāvānnāmarūpiṇām |
vāgādīndriyakāryasya tadā kasyāpyayogataḥ || 499 ||
nendriyāṇi tadā santi tadatra prāṇaśabdyatā |
naiṣāṃ , kintu parasyaiva , prasiddhiścātra vaidikī || 500 ||
|| iti prāṇotpattyadhikaraṇam ||
|| saptagatyadhikaraṇam || 
sapta gaterviśeṣitatvācca|| 
samastānāmindriyāṇāṃ kāryatvaṃ prāṅnirūpitam |
teṣāṃ saṅkhyā'trādhikṛtau vicārya sthāpyate nayaiḥ || 501 ||
sapta prāṇā iti śrutyā gatirjīvātmanā saha |
saptānāmevocyate'tha yadā pañce ti vākyataḥ|| 502 ||
eteṣāmeva yoge ca viśiṣya vacanaṃ tataḥ |
pañca śrotrādīni budhirmana etāni sapta vai || 503 ||
indriyāṇi , na vāgādīnyātmanā gatyabhāvataḥ
alpopakārakeṣveṣu prāṇoktistvaupacārikī || 504 ||
hastādayastu sthite'to naivam || 
jīve sthite śarīrāntaḥ , hastādīnyapi pañca hi |
tasyopakaraṇāni syuḥ boddhyaṃ saptaiva netyataḥ || 505 ||
daśeme paruṣe prāṇāḥ , indriyāṇi daśeti ca |
śrutismṛtibhyāṃ jīvopakaraṇatvāviśeṣataḥ || 506 ||
pañca śrotrādīni pañca hastādīnyekakaṃ manaḥ |
ekādaśendriyāṇi syuḥ nyūnasaṃkhyoktiratra hi || 507 ||
tattadvyāpāramātrasya tatra tatra vivakṣayā |
yogakāle viśiṣyoktiḥ śrotrādermanaādinā || 508 ||
jñānendriyatvaprādhānyāt daśaikaṃ cendriyāṇyataḥ |
saptaivetyuktiniyamo na syātanmānavirodhataḥ || 509 ||
|| iti sapragatyadhikaraṇam ||
|| prāṇāṇutvādhikaraṇam || 
aṇavaśca || 
saṃkhyā nirdhāritā pūrva indriyāṇāṃ pramāṇataḥ |
atrādhikaraṇe teṣāṃ parimāṇaṃ nirūpyate || 510 ||
sarve'nantā iti śrutyā prāṇānanatyāvabodhanāt |
vibhutvameṣāṃ siddhaṃ syāt iti vādo'samañjasaḥ|| 511 ||
sarve prāṇā anūtkrāmantī ti gatyupadeśataḥ |
siddhe satyalpamānatve cāpratyakṣatvato'ṇavaḥ || 512 ||
tadānantyaśrutistāvat kāryānantyāvabodhikā |
śreṣṭhaśca 
śarīrasthitahetutvāt śreṣṭatvaguṇasaṃyutaḥ || 513 ||
prāṇo na jāyate sṛṣṭeḥ prāk svakāryasthitiśruteḥ |
jīvasyevāsya cotpattiśrutirneyānyatheti ceta || 514 ||
mukhyaprāṇopīndriyavadutpadyata itīryate |
etasmājjāyate prāṇa ityutpattiśrutestathā || 515 ||
jīvasyevāsya cotpattiniṣedhāśravaṇāttathā |
prāksṛṣṭeśca paraikatvāvadhāraṇavirodhataḥ || 516 ||
ānīdavāta mityeṣā śrutistu prāṇanasya ca |
pralaye prāṇakāryasya sadbhāvaṃ na bravīti hi || 517 ||
parabrahmaṇa ekasya vakti sā vidyamānatām |
avātamiti tatraiva vāyvabhāvasya bhāṣaṇāt || 518 ||
|| iti prāṇāṇutvādhikaraṇam ||
|| vāyukriyādhikaraṇam || 
na vāyukriye pṛthagupadeśāt || 
mukhyaprāṇopīndriyavadutpadyata itīritam |
tasya svarūpaṃ prāṇasya vicāryātra nirūpyate || 519 ||
śreṣṭhaprāṇo vāyumātraṃ yaḥ prāṇaḥ sa iti śruteḥ |
bhūtādvitīye prāṇatvaprasiddhirneti cettadā || 520 ||
vāyorucchvāsādirūpakriyā vā prāṇa ucyatām |
iti pakṣadvayamapi na mānānuguṇaṃ bhavet || 521 ||
na vāyustakriyā vāpi prāṇo bhavitumarhati |
dehādidhṛtiyogyatvadaśāvān vāyureva saḥ || 522 ||
etasmājjāyate prāṇo manassarvendriyāṇi ca |
khaṃ vāyuriti vāyośca prāṇasya pṛthaguktitaḥ || 523 ||
na vāyumātraṃ prāṇaḥ syāt tata eva na tatkriyā |
kriyāyāḥ pṛthaguktirna cejaādiṣu dṛśyate || 524 ||
avaprāṇo vāyuvikāraścet tejo bhūtāntaraṃ yathā |
tathā'yamapyanyabhūtaṃ syādityāśaṃkya kathyate || 525 ||
cakṣurādivattu tatsahaśiṣṭyādibhyaḥ|| 
cakṣuḥśrotrādivatprāṇo jīvopakaraṇaṃ matam |
tathopakaraṇatvaṃ ca tādṛśairindriyaissaha || 526 ||
prāṇasaṃvādādike'sya śāsanādavagamyate |
tatsājātye hi satyeva pāṭhastaissaha yujyate || 527 ||
karaṇeṣu viśiṣyektyāpyayamartho'vagamyate |
tasmādagnyādivannāyaṃ prāṇo bhūtāntaraṃ bhavet || 528 ||
akaraṇatvācca na doṣastathāhi darśayati || 
jīvaṃ pratyupakārātmakriyāśūnyatvato'sya ca
udbhāvyate yaśca doṣaḥ na bhavet sa yataḥ śrutiḥ|| 529 ||
yasminnutkrānta iti ca pāpiṣṭataramityapi |
śarīrendriyadhṛtyākhyakriyāṃ darśayati sphuṭam || 530 ||
jīvopakārakatvena śarīrādidhṛterataḥ |
prāṇasyaitasyendriyavat karaṇatvaṃ tadakṣatam || 531 ||
pañcavṛttirmanovadvyapadiśyate || 
yathā kāmādi manasaḥ kāmaḥ saṅkalpa ityataḥ|
vṛttitatkāryayorbhede'pyanyatattvaṃ na saṃmatam || 532 ||
tathā prāṇo'pāna iti girā prāṇasya vai gataḥ |
vṛttibhedamapānādirnānyatattvamiti sthitam ||
|| iti vāyukriyādhikaraṇam ||
|| śreṣṭhāṇutvādhikaraṇam || 
aṇuśca || 
avasthāntaramāpanno vāyurvai prāṇaśabditaḥ |
ityuktaṃ pūrvamasyātra parimāṇaṃ nirūpyate || 534 ||
prāṇonūtkrāmatī tyādavutkrāntiśravaṇādayam |
mukhyāprāṇopīndriyavadaṇurānantyabodhikā || 535 ||
sama ebhistribhirlokai rityādyā śrutirasya ca |
tadadhīnākhilaprāṇisthityutkarṣaṃ bravīti hi || 536 ||
|| iti śreṣṭhāṇutvādhikaraṇam ||
|| jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānādhikaraṇam || 
dyotirādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ tu tadāmananātprāṇavatā śabdāt || 
evaṃ saprāṇendriyāṇāmutpattyādi nirūpitam |
agnyādestadadhiṣṭhāne nirapekṣatvamasyate || 537 ||
jīvasya cāgnyādīnāṃ cendriyasaṃghapravartanam |
svāyattaṃ nairapekṣyādityuktirmānanirākṛtā || 538 ||
jīvātmanā sahāgnyāderindriyādipravartanam |
saṅkalpādeveśitussaṃbhavati śrutivākyataḥ || 539 ||
yo'gnau tiṣṭhan yamayati , sa ātme tyādikā śrutiḥ|
tasya ca nityatvāt || 
parātmādhiṣṭhitatvasya sarveṣāṃ niyatatvataḥ|| 540 ||
tatsaṅkalpādeva teṣāmadhiṣṭhātṛtvamucyate |
tatsṛṣṭetyādineśasya niyantṛtvena sarvadā || 541 ||
cidacitsu praveśo hi śrūyate smaryate'pi ca|
viṣyabhye tyādi , tasmātsyādarthaḥ proktaḥ samañjasaḥ|| 542 ||
|| iti jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānādhikaraṇam ||
|| indriyādhikaraṇam || 
ta indriyāṇi tadvyapadeśādanyatra śreṣṭhāt || 
śreṣṭhasya cakṣurādeśca jīvopakaraṇatvataḥ |
sarveṣāmindriyatvaṃ syāditi vādo'tra vāryate || 543 ||
karaṇatvātprāṇaśabdavācyatvādaviśeṣataḥ |
te sarva evendriyāṇi bhaveyuriti cenna tat || 544 ||
śreṣṭhātiriktāḥ prāṇāsta indariyaṇyeṣu vai sphuṭam |
indriyāṇi daśaikaṃ ce tyādinendriyatoktitaḥ || 545 ||
bhedaśrutervailakṣaṇyācca || 
etasmājjāyate prāṇo manassarvendriyāṇi ca |
ityādiṣvindriyebhyo'sya prāṇasya pṛthaguktitaḥ || 546 ||
indriyatvaṃ tadanyeṣāmeveti jñāyate sphuṭam |
manasaḥ pṛthaguktasya tvindriyatvaṃ pramāṇataḥ || 547 ||
śreṣṭhasya cakṣurādeśca vailakṣaṇyācca nāsya tat |
suṣuptau vṛttirasyāsti nendriyāṇāṃ hi dṛśyate || 548 ||
samanaścakṣurādestu kāryaṃ jñānādikaṃ bhavet |
dehendriyadhṛtistvasya tasmānnendriyatā'sya hi || 549 ||
|| itīndriyādhikaraṇam ||
saṃjñāmūrtiklṛptyadhiraṇam || 
saṃjñāmūrtiklṛptistu trivṛtkṛrvata upadeśāt || 
samaṣṭisṛṣṭirbrahyaikakṛteti prāṅnirūpaṇāt |
niścitaṃ brahmakāryatvaṃ mahābhūtondriyādiṣu || 550 ||
devādivyaṣṭimṛṣṭiryā caturmukhakṛtaiva sā |
iti śaṅkāpanodena devamartyādidehinām || 551 ||
tatkāryatāniścayāya pravṛtteyamadhikriyā |
nāmarūpavyākriyātmaprapañcavyaṣṭisarjanam || 552 ||
samaṣṭijīvarūpalya dhātureva kriyā bhavet |
anena jīvene tyādivyatirekaśrutesyathā || 553 ||
atra vyākaravāṇī ti hyuttamoktiḥ kathañjana |
jīvātmanaḥ parāṃśatvāt upapadyata eva ca || 554 ||
tasmādetadvyākaraṇaṃ dhātureveti cenna tat |
trivṛtkurvata evaitadvyākṛterupadeśataḥ || 555 ||
nāmarūpavyākaraṇavyaṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ parātmanaḥ |
trivṛtkaraṇametattu dhātrā kartuṃ na śakyate || 556 ||
caturmukhajanirhyaṇḍasṛṣṭyanantarabhāvinī |
trivṛtkṛtaireva tejobannestatsṛṣṭisaṃbhavaḥ || 557 ||
tasmin jajñe svayaṃ brahamā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ |
sametyānyonyasaṃyogamaṇaḍamutpādayanti te || 558 ||
ityādi smaryate'to vai caturmukhaśarīrakāt |
paramātmana evaiṣā vyāṣṭisṛṣṭiritīryate || 559 ||
śrutau vyākaravaṇīti nirdeśaśca susaṅgataḥ |
anena jīvene tyādi vyatirekavacoṣa'pi tat || 560 ||
jīvātmadehakenātmanetyarthāddhi susaṅgatam |
jīvaśabdastejaādiśabdavajjīvadehakam || 561 ||
brahma vakti tato jīvenātmaneti śrutau śrutam |
sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca mukhyaṃ nirdiṣṭayoriha || 562 ||
praveśavyākaraṇayorekakartṛkatāpi ca |
nāmarūpavyākaraṇaṃ kriyā'taḥ paramātmanaḥ || 563 ||
trivṛtkāraprakāro'tra vedhassṛṣṭeṣu vastuṣu |
pradarśayate'nnamaśitaṃ tredhaṃ tyādyairataśca saḥ || 564 ||
vedhaḥkṛtastrivṛtkāraḥtat evopapadyate |
nāmarūpe vyākaro ditye kaikāmakaro diti || 565 ||
paurvāparyamapītyetadvaco'tra vinivāryate |
māṃsādi bhaumaṃ yathāśabdamitarayośca || 
annamityādinā nātra trivṛtkaraṇamucyate || 566 ||
bhuktānnādeḥ triprākāraḥ pariṇāmo'tra kathyate |
yaḥ sthaviṣṭho bhāgaḥ evamādiparyāyataḥ kramāt || 567 ||
purīṣaṃ māṃsamanasī vikārā annavastunaḥ |
mūtrāsṛjī prāṇa ime pariṇāmā jalasya ca || 568 ||
asthimajjāvāca iti vikārāstedajaso'pi ca |
yadīha cejobannānāṃ trivṛtkaraṇamucyate || 569 ||
tarhi bhaumāt purīṣāddhi māṃsasya manasaḥ kramāt |
aṇutvādāpyatāṇīyastvācca taijasatā''patetaḥ || 570 ||
pṛthivyaptejasāmatrivṛtkṛtānāṃ yataḥ kramāt |
sthaviṣṭhatvāṇutāṇīyastvasvabhāvo hi saṃmataḥ || 571 ||
āpaḥ pītāstathā tejo'śitamityādyupakramāt |
tayoḥ paryāyayoścaivamāpyataijasate kramāt || 572 ||
ucyete hi yathāśabdaṃ vākyaśeṣastathaiva hi |
āpomayaḥ prāṇa iti vāk ca tejomayī ti ca || 573 ||
prāgukto'tra trivṛtkāraprakāro yadi kathyate |
sthaviṣṭhatvādaṇīyastvāttanmūtraprāṇayoḥ kramāt || 574 ||
bhaumataijasate cāpi prasajyete yathākramam |
tathāsthimajjayoścāpi bhaumatā cāpyatā''patet || 575 ||
atastrivṛtkṛtasyaiva prāgannāderihocyate |
tredhā pariṇatistasmāt trivṛtkāro'tra nocyate || 576 ||
trivṛtkṛtirnāmarūpavyākṛteḥ pūrvabhāvinī |
balīyorthakrameṇātra pāṭharītistu bādhyate || 577 ||
yadagne rohita miti trivṛtkārapradarśanam |
aṇḍasthāgnyādiṣu , śvetaketoraṇḍasthitatvataḥ || 578 ||
tasmai bahirvastuṣu tadbhodhanāsaṃbhavādataḥ |
trivṛtkṛtānāṃ kāryeṣu kriyate tatpradarśanam || 579 ||
vaiśeṣyāttu tadvādastadvādaḥ || 
annamāpasteja iti pādanāṃ śuddhavācinām |
trivṛtkṛtapṛthivyādiparatvaṃ cātra yujyate || 580 ||
ekaikasya hi bāhulyādekaikasminnatha triṣu |
annādivāk , padābhyāso'dhyāyapūrteḥ pradarśakaḥ || 581 ||
|| iti saṃjñāmūrtiklṛptyadhikaraṇam ||
|| iti dvitīyādhyāye caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| iti dvitīyādhyāyaḥ ||
|| śrīrastu ||
|| śrīmate śrīrāghavāryamahādeśikāya namaḥ ||
|| asmadguruparamparāyai namaḥ ||
kadṛdvaddhadyaṇ ṝḍḍaṇnrṭhṭhanrṭhṭha
|| caturthādhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| arcirādyadhikaraṇam || 
|| arcirādinā tatprathiteḥ ||
hārdānugrahato nāḍīviśeṣāt sthūladehagāt |
utkrāntasya brahmavidaḥ prāk prokto gatyupakramaḥ || 189 ||
gacchatastasya viduṣo mārgo nirṇīyate'dhunā |
sarvaśrutiṣveka eva mārgo'rcirmukha ucyate || 190 ||
tenaiva vidvān kiṃ brahma yātyutānena vetaraiḥ |
anyatroktādhvabhirvā'to'niyamo'treti saṃśaye || 191 ||
mārgāṇāṃ naikarūpatvāt nairapekṣyānmithastathā |
atrāniyama eveti vacanaṃ naiva yuktimat || 192 ||
eka evārcirādissa mārgassarvatra kathyate |
tata ekenaiva tena pathā gacchati, tasya vai || 193 ||
sarvatra pratyabhijñānāt śrutiṣvadhvaika eva saḥ |
nyūnādhikatayā sarvaśrutiṣu pratipādyate || 194 ||
vidyāguṇopasaṃhāro yathā tadvadihāpi ca |
kāryaikatra śrutānāṃ ca tadanyatropasaṃhṛtiḥ || 195 ||
tatassarvopaniṣadi śrutasyaikatvato'dhvanaḥ |
tenaiva sarve vidvāṃso gantavyā iti susthitam || 196 ||
|| ityarcirādyadhikaraṇam ||
|| vāyvadhikaraṇam ||
|| vāyumabdādaviśeṣaviśeṣābhyām ||
arcirādiprakaraṇe sāmni saṃvatsarātparam |
ādityaḥ śrūyate'nyatra devalokaśca mārutaḥ || 197 ||
tayormadhye śrutāvetau bhinnārthāvathavā marut |
eka eveti viśaye, bhinnārthau śabdabhedataḥ || 198 ||
bhinnārthatve cordhvaśabdāt pañcamyā ca tayordvayoḥ |
saṃvatsarādityamadhye prāptatvāt śrutirītitaḥ || 199 ||
viśeṣābhāvataścāsau vidvān gacchet svakecchayā |
naivaṃ saṃvatsarādūrdhvaṃ vāyuṃ gacchedasau pumān || 200 ||
aviśeṣaviśeṣābhyāṃ vāyoreveritatvataḥ |
devalokapadaṃ tvatra devānāṃ loka ityataḥ || 201 ||
sāmānyenāha vāyuṃ taṃ sa vāyumabhigacchati |
iti vāyupadaṃ tvatra taṃ viśeṣeṇa vakti ca || 202 ||
atastābhyāṃ ca śabdābhyāṃ vāyurevābhidhīyate |
atassaṃvatsarādūrdhvaṃ gacchettaṃ vāyumeva saḥ || 203 ||
āvāsabhūto devānāṃ vāyurityucyate śrutau |
yo'yaṃ pavata ityatra tasmāduktārthasaṅgatiḥ || 204 ||
|| iti vāyvadhikaraṇam ||
|| varuṇādhikaraṇam ||
taṭito'dhi varuṇassaṃbandhāt || 
chāndogyādiṣvarcirāderadhvanaḥ krama īritaḥ |
tatra saṃvatsarādūrdhvaṃ siddhaṃ vāyorniveśanam || 205 ||
vāyorūrdhvaṃ yathāpāṭhaṃ kimete varuṇādayaḥ |
niveśyā uta sarvānte taṭito'dhīti cintane || 206 ||
kauṣītakyanusāreṇa vāyorūrdhvaṃ pracetasaḥ |
niveśo'taśca vāyvarkakramabādhānmarutvataḥ || 207 ||
prajāpateśca tatraiva praveśa iti cenna sat |
meghavartitvasaṃbandhāt vidyudvaruṇayormithaḥ || 208 ||
vidyuto'dhi niveśo'sya varuṇasyopapadyate |
tataḥ paścādindradhātroḥ niveśo varuṇādadhi || 209 ||
paṭhanādāgantukānāmante sthāgyatvato'pi ca |
evaṃ cāyaṃ kramassiddhaḥ ātivāhikasantateḥ || 210 ||
arcirdinādyapakṣodagayanābdasamīraṇāḥ |
sūryacandrataṭitpāśiśakrābjaprabhavā iti || 211 ||
|| iti varuṇādhikaraṇam ||
|| ātivāhikādhikaraṇam || 
|| ātivāhikāstalliṅgāt ||
kimarcirādayo mārgacihnabhūtā ime'thavā |
bhogabhūmaya ete kiṃ viduṣāmātivāhikāḥ || 212 ||
cintāyā cihnabhūtāste grāmādīnprati laikikaḥ |
mārgopadeśa evaṃ hi dṛśyate kālavācinām || 213 ||
eteṣāṃ cihnabhāvasyā'yogāt syurbhogabhūmayaḥ |
aharādereta evetyatra lokatvasaṃśruteḥ || 214 ||
lokaśabdenārcirādīn paṭhantyeke ca śākhinaḥ |
naivaṃ mārgātivoḍhāro niyuktāḥ paramātmanā || 215 ||
ganturgamayitṛtvātmaliṅgato viduṣo'dhvani |
amānavassa enānityupasaṃhāravākyataḥ || 216 ||
vaidyutasyātivoḍhṛtvadarśanāt pūrvavartinām |
itareṣāṃ cāviśeṣanirdiṣṭānāṃ sa eva ca || 217 ||
ativoḍhṛtvasaṃbandha iti niścīyate tataḥ |
bhogābhāvānmārgamadhye mārgavoḍhṛtva eva hi || 218 ||
lokaśabdo yujyate'tra śabdāstvete'rcirādayaḥ |
tadātmabhūtāśśaṃsanti devatā abhimāninīḥ || 219 ||
vaidyutasyaiva puṃso'tra brahmaprāpakatāśruteḥ |
pareṣāṃ varuṇādīnāṃ ātivāhikatā katham || 220 ||
|| vaidyutenava tatastacchateḥ || 
vidyutaścordhvameteṣāṃ gamanaṃ vaidyutena vai |
sa enāniti tasyaiva brahmaprāpakatāśruteḥ || 221 ||
amānavasya tasyaiva pāśyādyāssahakāriṇaḥ |
tato'rcirādayassarve viduṣāmātivāhikāḥ || 222 ||
|| ityātivāhikādhikaraṇam ||
|| kāryādhikaraṇam || 
|| kāryaṃ bādarirasya gatyupapatteḥ || 
pūrvaṃ gamayitāraste proktā arcirmukhādhvani |
nirūpyante'tra gantāraḥ nirasya ca matāntaram || 223 ||
arcirādigaṇaḥ kāryamupāsīnān caturmukham |
kiṃ nayatyathavā brahmadhyātṝnevota tānapi || 224 ||
jīvaṃ brahmātmakatvenopāsīnāṃśceti saṃśaye |
upāsīnāneva kāryaṃ nayatītyāha bādariḥ || 225 ||
hiraṇyagarbhopāsīnasyaiva gatyupapattitaḥ |
sarvatra pūrṇaṃ tadbrahmopāsīnasya tadāptaye || 226 ||
deśāntaragatirnaiva yuktā prāptatvatassadā |
paravidyā kāryamatrā'vidyānāśanameva hi || 227 ||
paricchinnapradeśasthaprāptaye hi gatirmatā |
ataḥ kāryopāsitāraṃ nayatyarcirmukho gaṇaḥ || 228 ||
|| viśeṣitatvācca || 
puruṣo mānaso brahmalokāniti bahūktitaḥ |
lokaśabdācca dhātāramupāsīnamamānavaḥ || 229 ||
nayatīti jñāpyate'tha gato'rcirmukhamārgataḥ |
prajāpatessabhāṃ veśmetyabhisandhatta ātmanaḥ || 230 ||
vedhontikagatiṃ tasmādarcirādirnayatyamum |
niścite satyevamenān brahmetyabjabhuvaḥ katham || 231 ||
uktirnapuṃsakabrahmaśabdenetyatra kathyate |
|| sāmīpyāttu tadvyapadeśaḥ || 
vidhāturbrahmaśabdoktirvrahmasāmīpyato bhavet || 232 ||
asya prathamajatvena tatsāmīpyaṃ hi vidyate |
ābrahmabhuvanādityādyuktyā prāptasya vedhasam || 233 ||
atyājyā punarāvṛttirityatrottaramucyate |
kāryātyaye tadadhyakṣeṇa sahātaḥ paramabhidhānāt ||
hiraṇyagarbhaprāptāvapyanāvṛttiśca yujyate || 234 ||
kāryavedholokanāśe tadadhyakṣeṇa vedhasā |
viduṣo'syāpi te brahmetyatra brahmāptibhāṣaṇāt || 235 ||
|| smṛteśca || 
brahmaṇā saha te sarve saṃprāpte pratisañcare |
parasyānte kṛtātmānaḥ praviśanti paraṃ padam || 236 ||
iti smṛteścāyamartho jñāyate'to'rcirādikaḥ |
kāryadhyātṝneva nayatyevaṃ vadati bādariḥ || 237 ||
atra sūtratrayeṇāha siddhāntaṃ jaiminiḥ svakam |
|| paraṃ jaiminirmukhyatvāt ||
arcirādiḥ paraṃbrahmadhyātṝneva nayatyasau || 238 ||
brahmaśabdasya mukhyatvāt sa enāniti tatra vai |
gamanānupapattirna parasya brahmaṇassadā || 239 ||
sarvagatvepi viduṣo viśiṣṭasthānagasya vai |
sarvāvidyānāśapūrvabrahmāptiparaśāstrataḥ || 240 ||
lokaśabdabahūktibhyāṃ na pratītiḥ svayaṃbhuvaḥ |
niṣādasthapatinyāyāt yujyate karmadhārayaḥ || 241 ||
brahmaiva loka ityatrārthaikatve sati niścite |
bahūktiraditiḥ pāśānitivaccopapadyate || 242 ||
brahmaṇaḥ paripūrṇasya svecchayā parikalpitāḥ |
aprākṛtā anantāśca lokāssanti pramāṇataḥ || 243 ||
|| darśanācca ||
asmāccharīrādityādiśrutirarcirmukhādhvanā |
gatasya paramabrahmaprāptiṃ darśayati sphuṭam || 244 ||
|| na ca kārye pratyabhisandhiḥ || 
prajāpatessabhāṃ veśmetīyaṃ pratyabhisandhivāk |
hiraṇyagarbhe kārye na, dhūtvetyādiśrutau sphuṭam || 245 ||
abhisaṃbhāvyalokasyākṛtakatvābhidhānataḥ |
yaśohamiti sarvātmatvābhisandhānato'sya ca || 246 ||
tasmāt brahmopāsitārameva prāpayati dhruvam |
arcirādirgaṇa iti jaiminyākhyamunermatam || 247 ||
atra svasiddhāntamāha bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ |
|| apratīkālambanānnayatīti bādarāyaṇaḥ,
ubhayathāca doṣāt tatkratuśca ||
pratīkālambanebhyo'nyān nayatyarcirmukho gaṇaḥ || 248 ||
ityevaṃ bhagavānāryo manyate bādarāyaṇaḥ |
brahmadṛṣṭyā'thavā tattadrūpeṇopāsate'khilam || 249 ||
cidacidvastujātaṃ ye pratīkālambanā ime |
dhyāyanti ye paraṃ brahma yecātmānamupāsate || 250 ||
brahmātmakaṃ vinirmuktaṃ prakṛtyā dvividhā ime |
apratīkālambanākhyāḥ imānubhayadhā sthitān || 251 ||
nayatyarcirmukhastānna pratīkālambanān kvacit |
kāryapakṣe paraṃ jyotirityādiśrutyasaṅgatiḥ || 252 ||
parameveti niyame tadya itthamiti śrutiḥ |
kupyet pakṣadvaye doṣo nayati dvividhān tataḥ || 253 ||
atrāyamarthassiddhassyāt tatkratunyāyato'pi ca |
upāstau yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ prāpyaṃ tādṛśameva hi || 254 ||
pañcāgnividyāniṣṭhāste svarūpaṃ śuddhamātmanaḥ |
ya ātmanītyavagatabrahmātmakamupāsate || 255 ||
yatasteṣāmarcirādigamanaṃ śrūyate śrutau |
gatasya tena brahmāptiranāvṛttiśca kathyate || 256 ||
pratīkālambanānāṃ tu dvividhopāstyabhāvataḥ |
acinmiśropāsanācca tatkratunyāyato nahi || 257 ||
arcirādigatirbrahmasaṃprāptirata eva ca |
uktanyāyādeva teṣāmavarjyā punarāgatiḥ || 258 ||
pañcāgnyupāsane jīvo viśeṣyo yata eva hi |
tanniṣṭhānāṃ mārgamadhye kaivalyaprāptiriṣyate || 259 ||
yathākālaṃ ca tatsthānāt ūrdhvamarcirmukhā gatiḥ |
brahmaprāptiranāvṛttissidhyatyeṣā pramāṇataḥ || 260 ||
|| viśeṣaṃ ca darśayati || 
yāvannāmno gatamiti śrutirdarśayati sphuṭam |
pratīkadhyāyinā teṣāmarcirādyanapekṣaṇam || 261 ||
svalpaṃ phalaviśeṣaṃ ca, tasmāduktārthasaṅgatiḥ |
brahmaṇā saha te sarva ityuktiḥ puṇyakarmaṇā || 262 ||
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ taduparyapinītitaḥ |
tatraivābhyastavidyānāṃ brahmasaṃprāptisūcikā || 263 ||
tadapratīkadhyātṝn vai prāguktān dvividhānapi |
nayatyarcirmukhagaṇaḥ iti sarvaṃ samañjasam || 264 ||
|| iti kāryādhikaraṇam ||
|| iti caturthādhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| caturthādhyāye caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| saṃpadyāvirbhāvādhikaraṇam ||
|| saṃpadyāvirbhāvaḥ svenaśabdāt || 
pūrvapāde, prasthitasya viduṣo'rcirmukhādhvanā |
sūriṇā'mānavākhyena brahmaprāpaṇamīritam || 265 ||
caturthapādenaitena muktaiśvaryaṃ nirūpyate |
evamevaiṣa ityādivākyaṃ yat sāmani śrutam || 266 ||
kimanena svarūpāvirbhāvo jīvasya kathyate |
uta brahmopasaṃpattyā sādhyākāraparigrahaḥ || 267 ||
iti prāpte svarūpasya nirānandatvadarśanāt |
suṣuptyādau, tasya nityamāvirbhūtatvatopi ca || 268 ||
sukhaikāntāpūrvarūpasaṃbandho'nena kathyate |
abhiniṣpadyata iti vaco'tassaṅgataṃ bhavet || 269 ||
rasaṃ hyevāyamityasya sukhānantyamataśśrutam |
iti cenna paraṃ jyotissaṃpadyaitasya kathyate || 270 ||
svarūpāvirbhāva eva svenetyatra viśeṣaṇāt |
āgantukākārayoge vyarthaṃ taddhi viśeṣaṇam || 271 ||
tadabhāve'pi tasyātra svīyarūpatvasiddhitaḥ |
yaduktaṃ svasvarūpasya nityaprāptatvato vacaḥ || 272 ||
abhiniṣpadyata iti vyarthamityatra kathyate |
|| muktaḥ pratijñānāt ||
karmatatkṛtadehādimuktaḥ svābhāvikākṛtiḥ || 273 ||
abhiniṣpadyata iti śrutyā'tra pratipādyate |
ato nityaṃ svarūpasya prāptasyāpi hyavidyayā || 274 ||
tirohitasya tanmuktirabhiniṣpattirucyate |
tasyā eva pratijñānāt pratipādyatayā śrutau || 275 ||
tyaktakarmajadehasya tyaktāvasthātrayasya ca |
atra jīvasvarūpasya vaktavyatvena cāsakṛt || 276 ||
etaṃ tvevetyādivākye pratijñā hyatra dṛśyate |
svarūpamātrāvirbhāvaḥ puruṣārthaḥ kathaṃ bhavet || 277 ||
|| ātmā prakaraṇāt || 
svarūpeṇaivāyamātmā guṇāṣṭakavibhūṣitaḥ |
iti prakaraṇādeva jñāyate prāgudīritam || 278 ||
ya ātmeti prakaraṇaṃ jīvasyeti hi niścayaḥ |
atassvābhāvika tasya jīvasyedaṃ guṇāṣṭakam || 279 ||
pūrvaṃ tirohitaṃ brahmasaṃpattyāvirbhavatyatha |

yathā na kriyate jyotsnetyādigranthena hi sphuṭam |
ato yukto vikāsākhyāvirbhāvo bandhasaṃkṣaye || 291 ||
|| iti saṃpadyāvirbhāvādhikaraṇam ||
|| avibhāgena dṛṣṭatvādhikaraṇam ||
|| avibhāgena dṛṣṭatvāt ||
avirbhūtasvarūpasya jīvasya sthāna uttame |
paripūrṇeśānubhavaprakāro'tra vicintyate || 282 ||
kiṃ muktaḥ svaṃ pṛthagbhūtaṃ brahmaṇo'nubhavatyuta |
tatprakāratvena tadavibhaktamiti saṃśaye || 283 ||
so'śnute'tha yadā paśya ityādyupaniṣatsu ca |
smṛtau cedaṃ jñānamiti muktasya paramātmanā || 284 ||
sāhityasāmyasādharmyavyapadeśāttadīśituḥ |
vaṃspṛthagbhūtamevānubhavatīti vaco na sat || 285 ||

tattvamasyayamātmā'tha caitadātmyamidaṃ tathā |
ya ātmanītyādivākyaiḥ paramātmātmakaṃ sadā || 287 ||
taccharīratayā tasya prakāraḥ pratyagātmanaḥ |
svarūpamiti pūrvatra bahuśaḥ pratipāditam || 288 ||
atassadā'haṃ brahmāsmītyevānubhavatīśvaram |

tadguṇānubhavaṃ vaktītyatra bādho na kaścana |
ato brahmāpṛthaksiddhaṃ svaṃ mukto'nubhavatyasau || 291 ||
|| ityavibhāgena dṛṣṭatvādhikaraṇam ||
|| brāhmādhikaraṇam ||
|| brāhmeṇa jaiminirupanyāsādibhyaḥ ||
svarūpāvirbhāva uktaḥ tatsvarūpaṃ vicāryate |

caitanyamātramathavobhayaṃ taditi saṃśaye |
brahmasaṃbandhyapahatapāmmatvādiyutaṃ tathā || 293 ||

dahravākye guṇā ye hi brahmayogitayā śrutāḥ || 294 ||
upanyasyanta ete'smin jīve vākye prajāpateḥ |
ādinātra sa tatreti jakṣaṇādyuktirucyate || 295 ||
tasmāt jñānasvarūpatvaṃ na jīvasyeti tanmatam |
|| citi tanmātreṇa tadātmakatvādityauḍulomiḥ ||

svasiddhāntaṃ vadatyatra bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ || 299 ||
|| evamapyupanyāsātpūrvabhāvādavirodhaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ ||

bādho na dhīsvarūpatvaguṇāṣṭakayutatvayoḥ |
tulyapramāṇakānāṃ hi mitho bādho na yujyate || 301 ||
vijñānaghana eveti śrutau tatrāvadhāraṇam |
kṛtsnopyātmā jaḍānnityaṃ vyāvṛttaḥ svaprakāśakaḥ || 302 ||
anyāyattaprakāśo'sya svalpadeśo'pi netyamum |
arthaṃ vaktyatra dṛṣṭāntavākyādevaṃ pratīyate ||
jñāte lavaṇakhaṇḍe hi jihvayaikarase'pi na |

|| iti brāhmādhikaraṇam ||
|| saṅkalpādhikaraṇam ||
|| saṅkalpādeva tacchateḥ ||
guṇāṣṭakāvirbhāvaḥ prāk muktasyokto'tra cāntimam |
satyasaṅkalpatvaguṇamadhikṛtyā'tra cintyate || 307 ||

prayatnadarśanālloke muktasyāpi tatheti cet |

yatnasāpekṣatvavādiśrutirhyatra na dṛśyate |

|| ata eva cānanyādhipatiḥ ||

|| iti saṅkalpādhikaraṇam ||
|| abhāvādhikaraṇam ||
|| abhāvaṃ bādarirāha hyevam ||

muktasya dehendriyādi kimastyuta tat kimu |
yathecchamasti tannāsti ceti prāpte'tra saṃśaye || 316 ||
dehādyabhāvaṃ muktasya manyate bādarirguruḥ |

|| bhāvaṃ jaiminirvikalpāmananāt ||

|| dvādaśāhavadubhayavidhaṃ bādarāyaṇo'taḥ ||

|| tanvabhāve sandhyavadupapatteḥ ||
svenaiva sṛṣṭadehāderabhāve paramātmanā |

|| bhāve jāgradvat ||
sadbhāve nijasaṅkalpasṛṣṭatanvādivastunaḥ |

yatheśvaraḥ svayaṃ bhuṅkte sṛṣṭvā daśarathādikam |

athamātmābhimāno'ṇujīvasyānekavarṣmasu |
|| pradīpavadāveśastathā hi darśayati ||
yathā pradīpasyaikatra sthitasya svaprabhāguṇāt || 331 ||
anekadeśāveśaḥ syāt tathaikatra sthitātmanaḥ |
svaprabhā bhūta(rūpa)caitanyādāveśaḥ sarvavarṣmasu || 332 ||
yathā caikaśarīre'pi hṛddeśasthasya cātmanaḥ |

muktasyā''hāsya sārvajñyaṃ kathamityatra kathyate |
|| svāpyayasaṃpattyoranyatarāpekṣamāviṣkṛtaṃ hi ||
suṣuptimṛtyoranyatarāpekṣaṃ tadvacanaṃ, na tu || 335 ||

|| ityabhāvādhikaraṇam ||
|| jagadvyāpāravarjādhikaraṇam ||
|| jagadvyāpāravarjaṃ prakaraṇādasannihitatvācca ||

|| pratyakṣopadeśāditi cennādhikārikamaṇḍalasthokteḥ ||

|| vikārāvarti ca tathāhi sthitimāha ||

|| darśayataścaivaṃ pratyakṣānumāne ||

eṣa hyevānandayāti, māṃ ceti sphuṭameva te |

|| bhogamātrasāmyaliṅgācca ||

brahmānubhūtyākhyabhogamātre tatsāmyabhāṣaṇāt |

kadāciditi śaṅkāyāssamādhānamihocyate |
|| anāvṛttiśśabdādanāvṛttiśśabdāt ||

yathā sarvaguṇopeto jagajjanmādikāraṇam |

|| iti caturthādhyāye caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| iti caturthādhyāyaḥ ||
|| śāstraṃ ca parisamāptam ||
paraṃ śrīmānakhilajagadaṅgo'yamanaghaḥ
hitaṃ bhaktirnyāsaḥ phalamapi ca tatprāptiriti yaḥ |

atassaṃruddhaphalakapuṇyasyaivāptaṃsakramaḥ |
tasmādāśramadharmāste kartavyā viduṣā''darāt ||87 ||
|| ityagnihotrādyadhikaraṇam ||
itarakṣapaṇādhikaraṇam ||
bhogena tvitare kṣapayitvātha saṃpadyate ||
prāk puṇyapāpayornāśaḥ ukto'nārabdhakāryayoḥ |
ārabdhakāryeyornāśe viśeṣo'tra pradarśyate || 88 ||
phaladānapravṛtte kiṃ puṇyapāpe vinaśyataḥ |
vidyāyoniśarīrāvasāne'trāniyamo'thavā || 89 ||
taddehānte'nyadehānte veti prāpte'tra saṃśaye |
tasya tāva diti śrutyāṃ brahmaprāptavupāsituḥ || 90 ||
taddehapātāvadhikavilambasyaiva saṃśruteḥ |
taccharīrāvasāne vai tannāśa iti cenna tat ||91 ||
si phaladānapravṛtte bhogenaiva vināśya ca |
atha saṃpadyate brahma nāśāsaṃbhavatastayoḥ || 92 ||
phalabhogaṃ vinārabdhaphalayorekadehataḥ |
phalānubhava ityatraniyamāt yena varṣmaṇā || 93 ||
phalabhogasamāptiḥ syāt tadante nāśa etayoḥ |
tasya tāva cchatirabhipraiti karmavimokṣaṇam || 94 ||
ataścāniyamo nāśe prārabdhaphalayostayoḥ |
evaṃ cānārabdhaphalamabhuktaṃ prāganuṣṭhitam || 95 ||
anantaṃ cānādikālasañcitaṃ dvividhaṃ ca tat |
vidyāmahimato naśyatyanantarakṛtaṃ ca tat || 96 ||
prāmādikaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ tu na śliṣyati viduṣyatha |
naṣṭaṃ tadīyapuṇyaṃ ca pāpaṃ mitrārisaṅgatam || 97 ||
phalamasmai na dāsyāmi puṇyaduṣkṛtakarmaṇoḥ |
evaṃ parasya saṅkalpastayornāśa itīryate || 98 ||
mitrārisaṃkramaścāpi suhṛde viduṣo'sya ca |
dāsye puṇyaphalaṃ pāpaphalaṃ cāsyaiva śatrave || 99 ||
evaṃ parātmasaṅkalpaḥ dehe tiṣṭhati yoginaḥ |
vidyāmāhātmyasaṃbhūtaṃ phalaṃ pāde'tra cintitam || 100 ||
iti itarakṣapaṇādhikaraṇam ||
|| iti caturthā'dhyāye prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| caturthādhyāye dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
vāgādhikaraṇam ||
vāṅmanasi darśanācchabdācca ||
anuṣṭhitopāsanasya viduṣaḥ puṇyapāpayoḥ |
nāśarūpaṃ pūrvapāde vidyāphalamudīritam || 101 ||
mumūrṣorviduṣo muktilokādhvopakramātmakaḥ |
nāḍīpraveśo'tra pāde vidyāphalamudīryate || 102 ||
ādau śarīrādutkrantiprakāraḥ pratipādyate |
āmnāyate vāṅmanasi saṃpadyata itīha kim || 103 ||
saṃpadyate vāksvarūpaṃ manasīti nigadyate |
athavā vāgvṛttirevetyebaṃ prāpte'trtra saṃśaye || 104 ||
vāgindriyasya manasi layo na pratipādyate |
indriyasyaitasya manaḥkāryatvābhāvato'pi tu || 105 ||
vāgvyāpārasya manasi layaḥ procyata ityasat |
vāgindriyaṃ vai manasi saṃyujyata itīryate || 106 ||
vāgindriye'ssin śānte'pi manovyāpāradarśanāt |
śabdaśca vāksvarūpasya saṃpattāveva dṛśyate || 107 ||
akāraṇe layaibhāvāt saṃpattipadamatra hi |
saṃyogarūpārthamāha na layārthamasaṃbhavāt || 108 ||
ata eva sarvāṇyanu || 
yato manasi saṃyogamātraṃ vāco layo natu |
ata eva tadā vācamanu sarvendriyāṇi ca || 109 ||
hṛdi saṃpadyanta iti tasmādityevamādikā |
śrutiryuktā na hi hṛdi sarvendriyalayo bhavet || 110 ||
|| iti vāgadhikaraṇam ||
manodhikaraṇam || 
tanmanaḥ prāṇa uttarāt || 
manassarvendriyairyuktaṃ bhavet prāṇena saṃyutam |
uttarācca manaḥ prāṇa iti sāmani vākyataḥ || 111 ||
manaso'nnamayaṃ somya manaḥ ityādivākyataḥ |
pṛthvīprakṛtikānnopādānakāraṇakatvataḥ || 112 ||
prāṇasyā''pomaya iti śrutyā'pprakṛtikatvataḥ |
manasaḥ pṛthivīdvārā prāṇopādānavāriṣu || 113 ||
layaḥ syādityatra śaṅkā, tasyā eva samāhitiḥ |
mano'nnāpyāyitamabāpyāyitaḥ prāṇa īritaḥ || 114 ||
āpyāyanaṃ cātra kāryakṣamatāpādanātmakam |
na manaḥprāṇayorannābvikṛtitvamihocyate || 115 ||
manaḥ prāṇau hyahaṅkārākāśayorvikṛtī iti |
tasmātprāṇena manasaḥ saṃyogaḥ śrutisaṃmataḥ || 116 ||
iti manodhikaraṇam ||
adhyakṣādhikaraṇam || 
prāṇaḥ saṃyujyate tejasyuta jīve'tra saṃśaye |
śruteḥ prāṇastejasī ti tejasyeveti cenna tat || 117 ||
prāṇasya karaṇādhyakṣe jīvātmanyeva yujyate |
utkrāntau tasya jīvopagamādīnāṃ śruteriha || 118 ||
evamevetyupagamaḥ prāṇo'nūtkrāmatīti ca |
uktotkrāntiḥ pratiṣṭhā ca kasminnutkrānta ityapi || 119 ||
jīve saṃyujyā'tha tadyukprāṇastejasi yujyate |
iti prāṇastejasīti śrutirāha yathaiva ca || 120 ||
yamunāyā gaṅgayā saṃyujyā'bdhigamane'pi ca |
yamunā'bdhiṃ gacchatīti vacassādhu tathā'tra ca || 121 ||
ityadhyakṣādhikaraṇam ||
|| bhūtādhikaraṇam || 
bhūteṣu tacchateḥ 
prāṇasya jīvayuktasya saṃpattistejasīritā |
tejomātre'tha militabhūteṣveveti saṃśaye || 122 ||
tejomātraśrutestejasyeveti vacanaṃ na sat |
pṛthivīmaya ityādiśruterbhūteṣu yujyate || 123 ||
sarvabhūtamayatvaṃ hi ganturjīvasya kathyate |
ekaikasmin tejaādau saṃpattāvapi ca kramāt || 124 ||
udāhṛtaśrutiryuktā bhavedityatra kathyate || 125 ||
naikasmin darśayato hi || 
kāryākṣamatvādekaikabhūtasyaikatra tejasi ||
naiva saṃyujyate kāryākṣamatvaṃ ca śrutismṛtī |
spaṣṭaṃ darśayata stāsāṃ travṛtaṃ trivṛtaṃ śrutiḥ || 126 ||
sametyānyonyasaṃyoga miti smṛtirato bhavet |
tejastrivṛtkṛtaṃ tasmādbhūteṣveva sa yujayate || 127 ||
iti bhūtādhikaraṇam ||
āsutyupakramādhikaraṇam || 
samānā cāsṛtyupakramādamṛtatvaṃ cānupoṣya || 
vidvattadanyayośceyamutkrāntiḥ kiṃ samā bhavet |
utāviduṣa eveti viśaye viduṣo'tra vai || 128 ||
yadā sarve pramucyante ityatrāmṛtatāśruteḥ |
utkrāntyabhāvādanyasyā'viduṣasseti cenna tat || 129 ||
āgatvupakramāttulyā sotkrāntirviduṣo'pi ca |
nāḍyotkramya gatirhyasya śataṃ caike ti kathyate || 130 ||
yadā sarve pramucyanta ityādi śrutivākyataḥ |
śarīrendriyasambandhamadagdhvaiva yadāpyate || 131 ||
adhāśleṣavināśākhyāmṛtatvaṃ vai taducyate |
atra brahmāptivacanaṃ dhyānakālikatatparam || 132 ||
tadāpītessaṃsāravyapadeśāt || 
taccāmṛtatvaṃ pūrvoktarūpamevopapadyate |
ābrahmāptidaśāprāpteḥ saṃsāravyapadeśataḥ || 133 ||
arcirmukhena mārgeṇa gatvā deśaṃ vilakṣaṇam |
yāvat brahmāpyate tāvat dehayogākhyasaṃsṛtiḥ || 134 ||
tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanne tyādyairhyupadiśyate || 135 ||
sūkṣmaṃ pramāṇataśca tathopalabdheḥ || 
anuyāti yatassūkṣmaṃ śarīraṃ viduṣo'sya ca ||
ato bandho nātra dagdhaḥ tathā mānena gṛhyate |
kacchato devayānena saṃvādavacasendunā || 136 ||
taṃ pratī tyādinā sūkṣmadehavattopalaṃbhanāt |
sa cānuvartate tasmāt bandho dagdho'tra naiva hi || 137 ||
nopamardenāt || 
ataḥ sarve pramucyante kāmā yo'syā dikā śrutiḥ |
nāsya bandhopamardenāmṛtatvaṃ prabravīti hi || 138 ||
asyaiva copapatterūṣmā || 
vidyamānatvopapattessūkṣmadehasya kutracit |
mṛteḥ prāgviduṣaḥ sthūle kvacidūṣmopalabhyate || 139 ||
na cāyaṃ sthūladehīyo'nyatrāsyānupalaṃbhanāt |
tata ūṣmopalabdhissā tasyotkrāntinibandhanā || 140 ||
viduṣopi samotkrāntirāgatiprakramādataḥ |
pratiṣedhāditi cenna śarīrāt spaṣṭo hyekeṣām || 
viduṣaśca samotkrāntirityuktirnopapadyate ||
śrutau na tasya prāṇā ityutkrāntipratiṣedhataḥ |
si naivaṃ jīvādaviśleṣaḥ praṇānāmatra kathyate || 142 ||
na śarīrasya dehādutkrāntiratra niṣidhyate |
tasyā'kāmayamānasya prāṇāḥ svasmādupāsituḥ || 143 ||
notkrāmantyarcirādyadhvagatyarthaṃ saha santi hi |
utkrāntyapādānamagra jīva eveti ca śrutau || 144 ||
mādhyandinānāṃ spaṣṭo bi na tasmādi ti vākyataḥ
na viśliṣyantyataḥ prāṇāḥ ābrahmaprāptyupāsituḥ || 145 ||
smaryate ca || 
mūrdhanyanāḍyotkramaṇaṃ smaryate viduṣo'pi ca |
ūrdhvamekaḥ sthitasteṣāṃ yo bhitve tyādinā sphudam || 146 ||
ityāsṛtyupakramādhikaraṇam ||
parasampattyadhikaraṇam || 
tāni pare tathā hyāha || 
bhūtasūkṣmāṇīndriyaughaprāṇajībayutāni kim |
yathākarma yathāvidyaṃ svakāryāya prayantyatha || 147 ||
paramātmani yujyanta iti prāpte'tra saṃśaye |
madhye sukhādibhogasyādarśanātparamātmani || 148 ||
asaṃyujyaiva gacchanti yathākarmeti cenna tat |
tādṛṃśi bhūtasūkṣmāṇi saṃpadyante parātmani || 149 ||
tejaḥ parasyā miti hi śrutirāha yathāśruti |
kārya kalpyaṃ suṣuptyādau brahmayogena viśramaḥ || 150 ||
yathā tadvadihāpi syāt viśramasthānamīśvaraḥ |
bhūtasūkṣmāṇyato hārde saṃpadyanta iti sthitam || 151 ||
iti parasaṃpattyadhikaraṇam ||
avibhāgādhikaraṇam || 
avibhāgo vacanāt || 
sā kiṃ hārde'tra saṃpattirupādāne layātmikā |
utāvibhāgarūpeti cintāyāṃ paramātmanaḥ || 152 ||
kāraṇatvācca saṃpattivacanācca parātmani |
si kāraṇāpattireveti cenna vāṅmanasī tyataḥ || 153 ||
saṃpattivacanasyaiva sarvatrāpyanuṣaṅgataḥ |
anuktasyābhidhānavairūpyāsaṃbhavādapi || 154 ||
atrāpyaviśleṣa eva yukto'rtho'tastadā bhṛśam |
śrāntasya viśramāyaiva hārdasaṃpattirucyate || 155 ||
|| ityavibhāgādhikaraṇam ||
|| tadokodhikaraṇam || 
tadokograjvalanaṃ tatprakāśitadvāro vidyāsāmarthyāt taccheṣagatyanusmṛtiyogācca hārdānugṛhītaśśatādhikayā || 
viduṣo'viduṣaścaivaṃ tulyotkrāntirnirūpitā |
vidyādhīno viśeṣo'tra tasyāṃ viduṣa ucyate || 156 ||
śata caikāce tivākyaśrutā mūrdhaḥyayā tadā |
nāḍīśatādadhikayā nāḍyaiva viduṣo gatiḥ || 157 ||
anyāmirevāviduṣaḥ ityayaṃ niyamo'tra kim |
yukto na veti viśaye niyamassa na yujyate || 158 ||
nāḍīnāmatisūkṣmatvāt durvivecatayā bhṛśam |
upādātumaśakyatvāt puṃsā bhūyastvato'pi ca || 159 ||
śataṃ caiketi vākyaṃ tūtkrāntiṃ yādṛcchikīṃ tathā |
anuvaktīti cennaivaṃ hārdeśā''rādhanātmakāt || 160 ||
vidyāsāmarthyataścaitadaṅgagatyanucintanāt |
prītenānugṛhīto'sau hārdena bhavati kṣaṇāt || 161 ||
tato'syā sthānabhūtaṃ hṛt bhavatyagre prakāśavat |
evaṃ prakāśitadvāro hārdena paramātmanā || 162 ||
śatādadhikayā nāḍyā vidvānniṣkrāmati svayam |
nārāyaṇo hārda iti taittirīyādiṣu sphuṭam || 163 ||
|| iti tadokodhikaraṇam ||
|| naśmyanusārādhikaraṇam || 
raśmyanusārī || 
athaitaireveti vākya ādityakiraṇānasau |
anusṛtyaivordhvadeśaṃ yāti vidvānitīryate || 164 ||
yujyate niyamo'yaṃ kimava netyatra samśaye |
rātrau mṛtasya viduṣo raśmisamprāptyasambhavāt || 165 ||
na yukto niyamastattu pakṣaprāptaparaṃ vacaḥ |
naivaṃ raśmyanusāryeva yātyūrdhvaṃ brahmavittadā || 166 ||
etaireve tyavadhṛteḥ pākṣikatve'vadhāraṇam |
vyarthaṃ rātrāvapi raveḥ kiṇānusṛtirbhavet || 167 ||
grīṣme niśyapyūṣmalabdhyā rasmisattā hi dṛśyate |
hemante himabāhulyādūṣmā samyaṅna gṛhyate || 168 ||
nāḍīnāṃ kiraṇānāṃ ca śrūyate hi sadā'nvayaḥ |
ato'nusṛtyaiva raśmīnāpnoti brahmavit param || 169 ||
|| iti raśmyanusārādhikaraṇam ||
|| niśādhikaraṇam || 
niśi neti cenna saṃbandhasya yāvaddehabhāvitvāt darśayati ca 
rātrau mṛtasya viduṣo brahmasaṃprāptirasti kim |
uta netyatra cintāyāṃ divāce tyādinā smṛtau || 170 ||
niśāmṛtergārhitatvāt hetutvāccāpyadhogateḥ |
rātrau mṛtasyāsya naiva brahmāptiriti cenna tat || 171 ||
karmasaṃbandhasya yāvaddehabhāvitvato'sya ca |
siddhaiva brahmasaṃpattiḥ, vidyāpūrvakakarmaṇām || 172 ||
vidyāraṃbheṇa naṣṭatvāt aśleṣāduttarainasām |
antyadehāvadhitvāccārabdhakāryasya karmaṇaḥ || 173 ||
bandhahetorabhāvena brahmaprāptau na saṃśayaḥ |
tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣya iti śrutiḥ || 174 ||
darśayatyamumarthaṃ hi divā ce tyādikaṃ vacaḥ |
avidvatparameveti sarvamatra susaṅgatam || 175 ||
|| iti niśādhikaraṇam ||
dakṣiṇāyanādhikaraṇam || 
ataścāyane'pi dakṣiṇe || 
dakṣiṇāyana utkrāntaviduṣaḥ prāptirīśituḥ |
asti neti vicāre'tha yo dakṣiṇa iti śrutau ||
pare'yena mṛtasyāsya candrasāyujyabhāṣaṇāt |
tatprāptasyaitameveti punarāvṛttidraśanāt || 177 ||
bhīṣmādīnāmudaṅmāsapratīkṣādarśanādapi |
pare'yane sṛtasyāsya brahmāptirneti cenna tat || 178 ||
yato hetorniśi mṛtasyā pyuktā prāptirīśituḥ |
tata evaitasya bandhahetvabhāvātpare'yane || 189 ||
mṛtasyāpi parabrahmaprāptirbhavati tatkṣaṇāt |
candrasāyujyoktirasya viśramasthānatatparā ||
uktamāvṛttivacanamavidvadviṣayaṃ bhavet |
brahmaṇo mahimāptiśca vākyaśeṣe'sya kathyate || 181 ||
bhīṣmādīnāsudaṅmāsapratīkṣā yogabhūmataḥ |
svecchāmṛtīnāṃ tatkālaprāśastyodbodhanāya vai || 182 ||
dharmapravartanārthaṃ ca tasmāduktārthasaṅgatiḥ |
yatra kāle tvanāvṛttimāvṛttimiti ca smṛtau || 183 ||
mumūrṣūn prati vai kālaviśeṣaḥ pratipādyate |
ataścoktārthasāṅgatyaṃ kathamityatra kathyate || 184 ||
yoginaḥ prati smaryete smārte caite || 
devayānagatiryā ca pitṛyāṇagatiśca yā |
yogāṅgatvenā'nudinaṃ smartavye te ubhe gatī || 185 ||
uddiśya yoganiṣṭhān vai smaryete na mṛteriha |
mumūrṣoḥ smaryate kālaviśeṣo'dho hi śaṅkitaḥ || 186 ||
naite sṛtī pārtha ? jānan iti hyatropasaṃhṛtiḥ |
kālābhimānibhūyastvāt yatra kāla itīryate || 187 ||
ataśca dakṣiṇe niśyapyutkrānto brahmarandhrataḥ |
gṛhītārkakaro vidvān gatvordhvaṃ brahma gacchati || 188 ||
|| iti dakṣiṇāyanādhikaraṇam ||
|| iti caturthādhyāye dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
